# Ralph Progress Log

Started: 2026-10-24
Wave: paint-photocraft-wave1
---

## Codebase Patterns

- Similarity is scored by `scripts/ralph/parity.json` (PhotoCraft catalog + toolbox). A story flips only the ids in its `parity` array. Breadth before depth: no refinement stories while in-scope items are open.
- The window should match the PhotoCraft Pro screenshot: document tab, a control options bar (no hint sentences), one tool button per group, and the right dock stacked as Color, Properties, Navigator, History, Layers, Brushes. Chrome colors are overrides on `.paint-layout`, not edits to `brand.css`.
- A parity item is done only when a test runs the command and asserts pixels, a mask, the layer stack, or the view. An empty tab from US-224 is unfinished until that behavior exists.
- The only command an iteration runs is `node --test` on the story's own test file. Do not run `npm test`, eslint, prettier, `parity-score.mjs`, or `build-parity.py`. Flip the story's `parity` ids by hand.
- A `done: true` flag is not the feature. The Layers footer (link, fx, mask, adjustment, group, new, delete) has to stay on screen, and dock groups have to resize. Do not emit COMPLETE because the catalog is full. Do not append stories; when the queue is empty, stop.
- Chrome colors are the --pc-* tokens on .paint-layout (paint/style.css; tests/paint-chrome-colors.test.mjs). New chrome reads --bg-2 (panel), --bg-1 (tab strip / surround), --border, --fg, --accent; do not hard-code grays or brand surfaces.
- Paint is a static browser app. Pixel work lives in `paint/adjustments.js` and `paint/tools.js`. There is no server and no bundler.
- New adjustments are entries on `ADJUSTMENTS`. The Adjust menu and dialog already render `label`, `fields`, and `apply`. Do not build a second menu.
- Adjustment `apply` signatures are `(imgData, params, mask)`. A mask is a `Uint8Array` of 0/1, or null for the whole image. Skip pixels where `mask && !mask[p]`.
- Selection masks use the same 0/1 layout, index `y * W + x`. Combine them with `applySelectionWithMask(state, mask, W, H, mode)`.
- New selection tools must be added to SELECTION_TOOLS in paint/pointer.js and the two tool-switch lists in paint/paint.js.
- Marquee tools follow `rectSelect` / `lasso` in `paint/tools.js`: honor `selectionModeFor` (add / subtract / intersect), and call `tryBeginMove` before starting a new selection when no modifier is held.
- `scanlineFill` is the polygon rasterizer. Polygonal lassos should close the path and reuse it.
- Tests are `tests/*.test.mjs` run by `node --test` (`npm test`). Import paint modules directly. No Playwright, no browser, no network.
- Quality gate for a story: `npx eslint` on touched JS, `npx prettier --write` on touched files, then `npm test`.
- The hamburger nav is fixed at the top-left. New header controls must not sit under it. `nav.js` is loaded with `data-nav-size="compact"`.
- Image › Adjustments is IMAGE_ADJUSTMENT_COMMANDS in paint/commands.js (catalog order; buildAdjustMenu and #adjust-menu-list are gone). Dialog-less adjustments (Invert, Desaturate) are in NO_DIALOG_ADJUSTMENTS and run ctx.applyAdjustmentNow. A filter is a [suffix, adjustment id] row in FILTER_COMMANDS and lands in its Filter submenu by catalog order.
- Undo is `pushUndo(label)` in `paint/paint.js` before a pixel or layer mutation.
- Goal: make paint as similar to PhotoCraft as a static browser canvas app can be, including the window layout. PhotoCraft is reference-only. Do not embed its WASM build.
- Layout stories US-058 through US-062 are done. Keep /brand.css tokens.
- The Layers panel (renderLayerPanel in paint/layers.js) is header (one .layer-blend + .layer-opacity for the active layer), rows (eye, thumb, mask thumb, name), and a .layer-footer-btn[data-action] icon footer. Reorder is row drag (callbacks.onMove → moveLayerTo); Duplicate / Delete / Merge Down / Flatten are the row right-click menu (openLayerMenu). Do not put per-row controls back.
- Tool and history icons are Lucide SVGs copied from PhotoCraft assets/icons into paint/icons.js. Do not add emoji tool icons. The site favicon and the title emoji stay. US-075 and US-076 run before any new tool. .paintproj stays.
- When every story passes, stop. A person writes the next wave. Leave PSD byte-fidelity, CMYK, ML select-subject, content-aware fill, and tear-off panels alone.
- After US-247, the open wave is US-248–US-254 (Layers floor, options bar, Properties, Adjustments, Navigator, History, Brushes). Do not append past US-254.
- Commit on the PRD branch only. Never commit to master. Never push.
- Layers multi-selection is state.selectedLayers (Set of layer ids) + layerSelectionFor + layerAnchor; read it through stateLayerSelection(state) (paint/layer-multi.js), which falls back to the active layer alone when activeLayerIdx changed elsewhere. Commands acting on several layers build an index plan (mergeLayersPlan / mergeVisiblePlan / hiddenLayersPlan) and run applyMergePlan / removeLayersAt.
- npm test already fails in pacman/test/level-data.test.mjs, tests/emulator-consoles.test.mjs, and tests/sitemap-seo.test.mjs. Those failures are pre-existing. Commit the story when the new paint tests pass. Do not write another blocked note about them.
- US-001's working patch is at /tmp/ralph-US-001.patch. Reapply it if applyGaussianBlur is not in the tree.
- Tools that must respect the active selection need adding to the toolbar's "don't commit selection" list in paint/paint.js; a pasted selection can still float (sel.data/baseData) while mode is 'active', so clear them before writing pixels under it (see gradient).
- Layer tests: stub globalThis.document with a fake ImageData-backed canvas before importing paint/layers.js (jsdom has no canvas package).
- Multi-click tools keep `state.drawing` true between clicks so pointer.js keeps sending onMove; onUp must be a no-op, and setActiveTool / Enter / Escape / dblclick in paint.js must end the in-progress state (see polyLasso).
- Inscribed-ellipse distortions (twirl, pinch, spherize) go through `remapEllipse(imgData, mask, warp)` in paint/adjustments.js; warp(u, v, r, out) writes the source point in ellipse units. Do not copy the bilinear sampler.
- Whole-image distortions (ripple, polar, wave) go through `remapPixels(imgData, mask, map, edge = 1)`; map(X, Y, out) gets the pixel centre and writes the source point in pixel units. edge is PhotoCraft's Edge: 0 Wrap, 1 Repeat (default), 2 Transparent.
- PhotoCraft layer filters run through algo::apply_in (Image::read_clamped), so neighbourhood filters repeat edge pixels rather than reading transparency off-canvas.
- Levels tables are levelsTable(black, gamma, white, outBlack, outWhite) in paint/adjustments.js; per-channel Levels (and future Auto Tone / Color) go through applyLevelsChannels(imgData, params, mask) with flat <ch>Black… keys.
- Voronoi cell filters (crystallize, later pointillize) use crystallizeCellPoint / crystallizeOwner in paint/adjustments.js; they are f32 via Math.fround to match PhotoCraft owners.
- PhotoCraft fxutil `gauss_blur_n` / `gauss_box_radii` are gaussBlurN / gaussBoxRadii in paint/adjustments.js (f32 Float32Array buffer, n interleaved channels); boxPass is the shared running-sum line pass. Do not write another box blur.
- Shaped kernels (Shape Blur, later Lens Blur) use shapeBlurSpans(r, inside) and SHAPE_BLUR_INSIDE in paint/adjustments.js (PhotoCraft blur2.rs `Spans` / `shape_inside`, f32). Reuse them rather than rasterizing another kernel.
- Menus live in #menu-bar (paint/menu-bar.js owns open / close / hover-switch via initMenuBar and closeMenus). A new menu is a .menu-bar-menu with a .menu-bar-btn and an .action-menu-list; paint.js only wires item clicks. Do not add per-menu toggle or document-click handlers.
- The canvas column (.canvas-column in .paint-body) is #canvas-area then #status-bar (coords, size, zoom group). Status readouts go in #status-bar, not the header.
- CATALOG ids can repeat (edit.keyboardShortcuts is in Edit and Window › Workspace); CATALOG_BY_ID in paint/commands.js keeps the first row, which is the command's home menu.
- The right dock is #dock (paint/dock.js initDock / selectDockTab): each .dock-group has a [role=tablist] whose tabs point at [role=tabpanel] via aria-controls; panels toggle with `hidden`. Show fg/bg colors through ui.js showColor('fg'|'bg', hex) so the chip, the color input, and the Color tab stay in sync.
- Retouch brushes share paint/retouch.js: tipShape / tipReach / dabCoverage (PhotoCraft's round tip) and the accumulate-then-composite pattern (snapshot the layer on down, a Float32Array cov per stroke, every dab rewrites dst from the pre-stroke src). Reuse them; see cloneDab and cloneStampSegment in paint/tools.js.
- roundTipDab (paint/retouch.js, internal) takes reach and a tip(d) coverage function; softTip is the round tip, pencilDab the aliased one.
- Brush dabs walk a segment with dabSegment(x0, y0, x1, y1, step, dab) and write back through putBox(ctx, img, box) in paint/tools.js. Do not write another dab loop.
- Sequential-dab retouch tools (Sponge, then Blur / Sharpen / Smudge) edit a per-stroke working copy in dab order with perDab(coverage × strength, spacing) from paint/retouch.js; see colorDab and spongeSegment.
- Neighbourhood retouch brushes (Blur, Sharpen, later Smudge) keep an RGBA Float32Array working copy (0..1, alpha at index 3) and write it back with putWork(ctx, img, work, 4, box) in paint/tools.js; focusDab(…, filter, mask) runs a window filter (localBlur / localSharpen) per dab.
- A stroke's RGBA f32 working copy comes from rgbaWork(img) in paint/tools.js (Blur, Sharpen, Smudge). Smudge's carry state is smudgeStroke(...) from paint/retouch.js, one per stroke.
- Tool icons are names in ICONS (paint/icons.js, inner SVG markup copied from PhotoCraft assets/icons); iconSvg(name) builds the svg. Use PhotoCraft's tool_icon mapping in crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs to pick the name (Healing Brush is 'bandage', already in ICONS).
- History row icons come from historyIconName(label) in paint/history.js: TOOLS labels use the tool's icon, ADJUSTMENTS labels use 'sliders-horizontal', other pushUndo labels go in COMMAND_ICONS, and unknown labels fall back to 'diamond'. A new pushUndo label that is not a tool or adjustment needs a COMMAND_ICONS entry.
- Spot-heal-style tools that only gather coverage during the drag use coverageDab(cov, …) from paint/retouch.js and draw a trail on the overlay; window-based heals reuse dilateMask, spotSamplingWindow, coverBounds (internal), and healOver for the coverage composite.
- History Brush sources are state.historySource (Map, layer id → ImageData) in paint/paint.js: resetHistorySources() on init / new / open, snapshotHistorySource(layer) on add / duplicate and the History tab button, cleared by resizeStack and crop. A new command that creates a layer or changes the canvas size must keep that in step.
- Whole-canvas and layer transforms live in paint/transform.js (rotatedSize, rotateImageData). Free Transform should reuse its bilinear premultiplied sampler.
- A Free Transform box is the affine ft.t until a Skew / Distort / Perspective gesture turns it into ft.quad (toQuad in paint.js); quad drags are dragQuad / quadGesture and the pixels go through warpQuad in paint/transform.js.
- paint/transform.js has one bilinear(src, W, bx, by, bw, bh, u, v, clamp, acc) sampler (rotateImageData and transformImageData). While state.freeTransform is set, pointer.js routes canvas events to state.freeTransformPointer, and pushUndo / setActiveTool commit it first (undo cancels it). A new canvas command needs no extra guard if it calls pushUndo.
- coverDab (paint/retouch.js) is the one composite loop for Clone Stamp, History Brush, and Pattern Stamp: it takes at(x, y) → source byte index (or −1 to skip). A new "paint some source through the round tip" tool writes an at() sampler, not a loop.
- Pixel-level Hue / Saturation / Color / Luminosity blends are blendNonSeparable(mode, cb, cs) with setLum / setSat / blendLum in paint/retouch.js (PhotoCraft blend.rs, Rec. 601 lum, clip toward grey). BLEND_MODES in layers.js are canvas composite ops, not pixel functions; reuse these instead of writing another set.
- Layer masks live on the layer record (layer.mask Uint8Array W * H or null, layer.maskEnabled, layer.maskCanvas / maskCtx gray edit surface). After changing layer.mask call syncLayerMask(layer) (paint/layers.js); the composite reads layerSource(layer); pushUndo / undo snapshot the mask through layerSnapshot / restoreLayerSnapshot in paint/paint.js. A command that resizes the canvas must crop / turn masks too (cropMask in paint/layer-mask.js).
- Adjustment layers are layer records with kind 'adjustment', adjustmentId, params, and an always-present mask that is the edit target (paint/adjustment-layers.js). Anything that composites the stack must go through flattenToCanvas or compositeWithAdjustments; while one is visible, paint.js shows one composited canvas (syncCompositeView / scheduleComposite) and the layer canvases are hidden edit surfaces.
- The tool column is TOOL_GROUPS in paint/tool-groups.js (re-exported by tools.js); a new tool needs a slot there and its PhotoCraft key in TOOL_KEYS. Shortcuts go through toolForKey, not a letter map in paint.js.
- Structure commands (add / delete / reorder layers, canvas size) take a whole-stack undo entry: put the pushUndo label in STACK_UNDO_LABELS in paint/stack-history.js. remountStack in paint/paint.js restores layers by id.
- ADJUSTMENTS fields may be { type: 'checkbox', default: bool } or { type: 'color', default: '#rrggbb' } (renderFields in paint/adjust-modal.js); params stay flat keys so defaultAdjustmentParams and .paintproj need no change.
- Array-valued adjustment params (Curves points) are never mutated: paint/curve-editor.js helpers return new arrays and the editor reassigns params[key]. Undo, Cancel, stack snapshots, and .paintproj only shallow-copy params, so in-place edits would leak into them. IDENTITY_CURVE is frozen to catch that.
- Adjustments that work in linear light use srgbToLinear / linearToSrgb / mat3Apply in paint/adjustments.js (PhotoCraft Transfer::Srgb, convert.rs constants). Do not add another sRGB transfer pair.
- The Crop tool's turn is state.cropAngle (degrees, clockwise, about the frame centre); hit-test and resize through cropHitTurned / cropResizedTurned in paint/crop-frame.js, never plain cropHit, and pass the angle to drawCropOverlay. TOOLS.crop.cancel resets it.
- Value readouts beside the pointer (crop W / H, Angle) go through drawReadout(ov, rows, x, y, px) in paint/tools.js; do not hand-draw another stroke-then-fill label.
- 3D LUTs and ordered dither are lut3dSample(table, n, rgb, tetrahedral) / lutLattice(n, f) / bayer4(x, y) in paint/adjustments.js (PhotoCraft adjust.rs, lutfile.rs from_fn). ADJUSTMENTS fields may also be { type: 'select', options: [{ value, label }] }.
- Every fg / bg color change goes through showColor(which, color) in paint/ui.js; a control that previews the colors (the Gradient preset swatch) registers a repaint in swatchRepaint rather than adding hooks in paint.js. renderToolOptions runs optionCleanup for its container first, so a control that adds a document listener pushes its removal onto cleanup.
- ADJUSTMENTS fields may be { local: true } (a dialog-only selector, never written to params; both default-param loops skip it) and { group: { key, value } } (row shows only while that selector or params[key] equals value). See Selective Color's Colors select; Channel Mixer's Output Channel can reuse it.
- A dialog-only computed value (Channel Mixer's Total) is an ADJUSTMENTS field { type: 'readout', local: true, text(params), warn?(params) }; renderFields refreshes it on every edit. Do not hand-build DOM for one adjustment.
- A select option may carry { group: { key, value } } (renderFields syncOptions): it is listed only while that key matches, and a select whose value drops out falls back to its first listed option. Channel Mixer's Gray output uses it with group on params.monochrome.
- A local field's default may be a function of params (Photo Filter's Use / Filter start from params.color), and a select may carry onChange(params, value) to write flat params from a dialog-local choice; color inputs re-sync from params on every edit.
- Checkbox fields may carry onChange(params, value) too (Hue / Saturation Colorize); sliders re-sync from params after every edit, so a hook that rewrites another param needs no DOM code.
- Hue / Saturation range params are flat <range>Hue / <range>Saturation / <range>Lightness keys over HUE_RANGES (paint/adjustments.js); hueRangeWeight(i, deg) and rangeLightness(rgb01, l) are the PhotoCraft HueRange weight and range_lightness ports. Reuse them for any per-hue-range edit.
- Dialog histograms are histogramBars(h, width, height) from paint/adjustments.js drawn with fillHistogram / drawHistogram in paint/adjust-modal.js; renderFields takes levelsHistograms' [R, G, B, composite] as its 5th argument and channelHistogram(histograms, channel) picks one (RGB / master → composite). A select's change runs the afterSelect redraws.
- Gradient Map stops are params.stops [location, '#rrggbb'] handled by paint/gradient-stops.js (normalizeStops, gradientMapStops for the legacy startColor / endColor pair, sampleStops, moveMapStop, addStopAt, addStopInWidestGap, removeStop); gradientMapColor lives there and adjustments.js re-exports it. ADJUSTMENTS fields may be { type: 'gradient' } (renderGradientMapEditor in adjust-modal.js).
- The document measurement scale is state.measurementScale (paint/measurement.js: DEFAULT_MEASUREMENT_SCALE, measurementScaleFactor, normalizeMeasurementScale), reset in resetHistorySources and saved in .paintproj; Set Measurement Scale should write a normalized copy and call refreshToolOptions.
- Adjustment-layer Cancel and the Edit Adjustment Layer undo snapshot go through replaceParams (paint/adjust-modal.js), which drops keys an edit added; do not go back to Object.assign.
- Redraws that must follow every dialog edit (Threshold's level marker) go in renderFields' afterEdit list, which changed() runs before onChange. Draw markers with strokes so test stubs can tell them from one-bin-wide histogram fillRects.
- Pixel blend modes: blendRgb(mode, cb, cs) / blendChannel in paint/retouch.js cover every BLEND_MODES value (PhotoCraft blend_rgb, 0..1). Composite with blend.rs composite (see the mode path in fillLinearGradientStops); do not write another blend switch.
- Shape layers are kind 'shape' with layer.shape { path, fill, stroke, live } (paint/shapes.js); edits go through editShape / translateShape (never mutate) and renderShapeLayer. Shape geometry is livePath(live) (polygonPoints, roundedPolygonPath, rectPath, linePath); vector-mask or Combine Shapes work should reuse them.
- Anything that composites a layer mask reads compositeMask(layer, W) from paint/layer-mask.js (density + feather, cached on layer.maskComposite), not layer.mask; syncLayerMask drops the cache. Properties (paint/properties-panel.js) owns adjustment-layer editing; the adjust modal is only for destructive Image › Adjustments.
- View flags are state.extras / show / snap / snapTo / grid / pixelArtPreview (paint/snap.js; shows(state, key) is Extras && flag). A new overlay reads shows(); a new tool whose corner should snap goes in POINT_SNAP_TOOLS in paint/paint.js (pointer.js runs state.beginSnap / retargetSnap / snapped).
- The Window menu is #window-menu-wrap; a window.panel.<name> command runs ctx.showPanel(name), which selects #tab-<name>. While state.quickMask is set, editingMask() is true and activeCtx() is the Quick Mask's gray canvas, so a new pixel command that goes through activeCtx / pullMaskEdit works on the mask for free. Alpha channels are state.channels (never mutated; ops in paint/channels.js return new arrays).
- File › Save / Revert / Close state is state.fileName, state.dirty (set by pushUndo via fileDoc.markDirty), and state.fileSnapshot (Revert point) in paint/file-save.js createFileDocument; loadProjectData(data, fileName) makes an opened project the Revert point. New code that swaps the document must call fileDoc.opened / reset.
- Menu items and Ctrl/Cmd shortcuts are commands in COMMANDS (paint/commands.js, command(id, run, opts)); label, menu path, and shortcut come from PhotoCraft's CATALOG (paint/menu-catalog.js) by id. run(ctx) calls a method on commandCtx in paint.js, so add the method there; buildMenuBar (paint/menu-bar.js) places, separates, and enables items, and keydown resolves through commandForKey. Never hand-write menu HTML.
- Gradient presets are GRADIENT_PRESET_GROUPS in paint/gradient-presets.js ([{ name, presets: [{ name, stops, opacity }] }], stop colors 'foreground' / 'background' / '#rrggbb'); resolvePresetStops(preset, fg, bg) gives fresh opaque stops and resolvePresetOpacity(preset) the [location, alpha] stops the Gradient tool passes as alphaStops. Dialogs get the app's [fg, bg] from deps.swatches (renderFields' colors argument). Reuse them for the Gradient tool.
- Options-bar icon toggle rows are TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA type 'iconButtons' (options { value, label, icon, title }); renderToolOptions builds a [role=radiogroup] with id opt-<key>. Reuse it for PhotoCraft icons::button rows instead of a select.
- The Gradient Preset is TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA type 'gradientSwatch' (options { value, label, group, preset }); presetSwatchCss(preset, fg, bg) in paint/gradient-presets.js is the CSS preview, and renderToolOptions takes [fg, bg] as its 5th argument.
- Options-bar selection modes: selection tools call selectionMode(state) in paint/tools.js (resolveSelectionMode(state.selectionMode, state) from paint/selection.js; held modifiers win). The value is shared on state.selectionMode, not state.toolOptions; refreshToolOptions in paint.js passes and writes it.
- Options-bar fields may be type 'number' { min, max, step, default } or 'iconAction' { icon, title, patch(values) } (no default, skipped by defaultToolOptions); any field may carry disabledWhen { key, value }, and a select may carry onSelect(value) → patch of other keys. renderToolOptions re-renders itself after a patch, so tool code needs no DOM work.
- Options-bar text buttons (PhotoCraft widgets::secondary_button: Front Image, Clear) are TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA type 'action' { label, title, patch(values) } (.tool-opt-button); like iconAction it holds no value and defaultToolOptions skips it.
- An options-bar field may carry a static disabled: true (rectSelect's Anti-alias); renderToolOptions disables it like disabledWhen.
- An options-bar field may be type 'color' { label, default: '#rrggbb' } (renderToolOptions in paint/ui.js, an <input type=color>); pair it with shownWhen to show it only for a Custom choice (Crop Shield).
- disabledWhen may also be { key, values: [...] } (disabled when the key matches any). Erasing on the round tip is eraseDab in paint/retouch.js (per-stroke cov, alpha rewritten from the pre-stroke src); reuse its shape for brush-type tools.
- Crop frame geometry is pure in paint/crop-frame.js (cropHit / cropDrag / cropDraw / cropClamp, frame [x0, y0, x1, y1] in document px, handle reach CROP_HANDLE_PX / zoom); the pending frame is state.cropFrame and every canvas crop goes through cropCanvas(sx, sy, sw, sh) in paint/paint.js. An options-bar iconAction that runs a command patches a '_<tool>Action' key that refreshToolOptions' onChange intercepts (see '_cropAction').
- Region selection from pixels is floodSelectImage(img, x, y, tolerance, contiguous) in paint/tools.js (ImageData-like input); the Paint Bucket fills its mask with bucketFillImage. Sample All / All Layers options read state.sampleVisible(W, H) (flattenToCanvas of the visible stack); reuse it rather than another matcher.
- Round-tip stroke tools that build coverage with flow toward a stroke opacity share roundTipDab(W, H, cx, cy, radius, hardness, flow, mask, cov, write) in paint/retouch.js (eraseDab, paintDab); a new one passes a write(p, c) callback, not another loop.
- Stroke tools clip to the active selection through strokeSelectionMask(state, W, H) in paint/tools.js on down (mask or null, float dropped); pass it as the dab mask, and add the tool to the "don't commit selection" list in paint/paint.js.
- Background Eraser and Color Replacement share keepConnected (paint/retouch.js, PhotoCraft keep_connected) for the Contiguous limit; hotspotColor(pre, W, H, x, y) is the transparent-aware hotspot sample (null skips the dab).
- Session tool state that outlives a stroke lives on state (state.mixer from mixerState() in paint/retouch.js, created lazily on the first Mixer Brush down); Load / Clean run in onUp through mixerEndStroke.
- Options-bar values carry docSize: [W, H] (refreshToolOptions) for patch functions that need the document; a select may carry extraOption(cur) to show an off-list value. A patch that changes another key via a paint.js command must return that key too (applyPatch re-renders from its own copy).
- The Crop tool always holds a frame while active: state.cropDefault marks the untouched default (ensureFrame); a command that changes the canvas size while Crop is active must drop a default frame and call ensureCropFrame() in paint/paint.js (afterHistoryStep does this for undo / redo).
- Options-bar fields may be type 'text' (string value, empty allowed) and carry shownWhen { key, value } (listed only while that key matches; a select another field's shownWhen reads re-renders the bar). The Crop W x H fields cropW / cropH use both; cropToolTarget(state) in paint/tools.js is the commit's { w, h }, resampleImageData in paint/transform.js the resampler.
- A select's onSelect(value, values) may return a patch that sets its own key; renderToolOptions then applies it in the patch's key order. Lead a multi-key patch with a '_<tool>Action' key so paint.js runs one command before the per-key onChange calls (Crop's 'choose:<key>').
- The Crop frame is drawn through drawCropTool(ov, state) in paint/tools.js (guides from cropToolGuides, crop_overlay.rs); do not call drawCropOverlay directly from crop code.
- Crop box history is state.cropUndo / cropRedo (paint/tools.js cropStep / pushCropStep); a new crop gesture that changes the frame takes cropStep(state) at its press and calls pushCropStep(state, before) on release. undo() / redo() in paint.js try cropHistoryStep first.
- Perspective Crop geometry is pure in paint/transform.js (convexQuad, quadSize, homographyFromQuad rect → quad, invertHomography, applyHomography, perspectiveCropSize, perspectiveCropImageData); the pending quad is state.perspectiveQuad and the commit is commitPerspectiveCrop in paint/paint.js. Its W / H options read and write state.toolOptions.crop cropW / cropH.
- Freehand lasso release is finishLasso(ctx, ov, state, feather) in paint/tools.js (Lasso, Patch). A tool that draws a lasso on some drags delegates onDown / onMove to TOOLS.lasso and calls finishLasso on up. Patch heals are patchHeal(dst, src, W, H, mask, dx, dy, mode) in paint/retouch.js; drag offsets are patchOffset in paint/tools.js.
- Tools that keep a drawing on the overlay between strokes (Ruler) set TOOLS.<id>.overlay(ov, state); selection.js redraws it after the marching ants and a deselect through state.drawToolOverlay. Options-bar read-only values are TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA type 'readout' { label, text(values) }.
- An options-bar 'action' patch is applied after onChange and re-renders from its own copy, so a command that may be a no-op (Straighten Layer on a level line) decides in patch(values) what the bar shows rather than having paint.js re-render.
- File › Place Embedded / File Info / Layers to Files / Scripts helpers are pure in paint/file-extras.js (insertPngText, fileInfoTextEntries, emptyLayerIndices, flattenAllMasks, layersToFilesList, placeFit). PNG downloads go through canvasPngBlob(canvas, state.fileInfo) in paint/file-menu.js so File Info lands in tEXt; reuse it for any new PNG export.
- Compound canvas commands call rotateStack / cropStack in paint/paint.js (rotateCanvas / cropCanvas without commit or undo) under one pushUndo(label, true); pushUndo's second argument forces a whole-stack entry.
- Fill / Stroke pixels are fillMask(img, mask, src, { mode, opacity, preserveTransparency }) and strokeMask / strokeBand in paint/fill-stroke.js (blend.rs composite / composite_atop over blendRgb; src is byte RGBA or a pattern tile). Reuse fillMask for any "blend a color or pattern through a coverage mask" command; squaredDistance (paint/selection.js) is the EDT.
- Layer transforms in document coordinates are transformLayerPixels(img, mask, box, affine, { nearest, fill }) / transformMask in paint/transform.js; transformActiveLayer(label, affineFor, nearest) in paint.js is the command wrapper (floating paste stamped, selection outline moved, state.lastTransform recorded for Transform Again). A new layer-moving command passes an affine, not another pixel loop.
- Picked colors go through pickColor / state.pickColor in paint/paint.js (docColor in paint/image-mode.js) so Grayscale mode clamps them; one-of-N menu items are commands with radio: true + checked(state).
- Mode conversions that flatten (Indexed Color, Bitmap) go through flattenToMode / collapseStack in paint/paint.js; state.colorTable rides in undo entries and .paintproj next to docMode.
- Status-bar notes (a command that did nothing and says why) go through showStatusMessage(text) in paint/paint.js (#status-message, clears after 4 s); do not use alert().
- Image › Adjustments that need a neighbourhood or histogram (Shadows/Highlights, Replace Color, Equalize; later HDR Toning, Match Color) live in paint/tone.js (PhotoCraft algo tone.rs), registered on ADJUSTMENTS; a color field with swatch: 0 / 1 is seeded from fg / bg when openAdjust opens.
- Count tool markers are state.count { groups, active } (paint/count.js, immutable helpers: addCountMarker / nearestCountMarker / removeCountMarker / moveCountMarker / clearCount), reset in resetHistorySources and saved in .paintproj; like the ruler they show on the overlay only while their tool is active and take no undo.
- The Background is a pixel layer with background: true, only ever layers[0] (paint/layer-new.js isBackgroundLayer / hasBackground / unlockBackground / canMoveLayer). Code that fills uncovered pixels with the background colour checks isBackgroundLayer(layer), never `layers[0]` or index 0; an edit that needs transparency on it calls unlockBackground (state.unlockBackground in tools) after pushing undo.
- Layer groups are kind 'group' records in the flat state.layers, directly above their children; every layer has parentId (null at top level). Edit the tree through paint/layer-groups.js (layerTree / normalizeTree, blockIndices for a group with its contents, moveLayerPlan + applyMovePlan, groupLayersPlan, ungroupPlan, reparentOrphans after removals); a new layer inserted above the active one takes its parentId. Anything that composites goes through flattenToCanvas or compositeTree (pass-through vs isolated groups); pixel commands guard with refuseGroupTarget in paint.js or pixelTarget in commands.js.
- Clipping and locks live in paint/layer-locks.js: layer.clipped (clips to the nearest unclipped sibling below; compositeTree gathers base + clipped and needs ops.isolate / ops.drawAtop on any new backend) and layer.locks { transparency, pixels, position, all } read through pixelsLocked / positionLocked / transparencyLocked (group locks included). Pixel commands refuse through refuseGroupTarget(what, lock) in paint.js ('pixels' default, 'position' for transforms, null for neither); a command that writes pixels and must keep alpha under Lock Transparent Pixels runs preserveAlpha(before, after).
- Arrange / Reverse are plans from paint/layer-arrange.js (arrangePlan, reversePlan → applyArrangePlan, nothing written until applied); Align / Distribute return [{ id, dx, dy }] from content rects { x0, y0, x1, y1 } (alignMoves, distributeMoves) and paint.js applyLayerMoves shifts every pixel layer in a moved group. An options-bar button that runs a menu command sends { _layerCommand: id } (TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA.select, iconMenu in ui.js).
- Layer styles are layer.styles (paint/layer-style.js); both compositors (layers.js canvasCompositeOps, layer-groups.js byteCompositeOps) render them through renderEffectParts. Its `under` list (Drop Shadow, Outer Glow) goes onto the backdrop each in its own mode; interior effects go on `body` through overlayColor(…, cov), which keeps alpha. New layer fields must also go in copyLayerContents, stack-history snapshot/copyRecord, paint.js remountStack, and project.js layerFlags / deserialize.
- Fill layers are kind 'fill' records with layer.fill (paint/fill-layers.js: { type: 'solid' | 'gradient' | 'pattern', … }); their canvas holds pixels rendered by renderFillLayer, so compositors treat them as pixel layers. refreshLayerPanelUI re-renders any whose canvas size changed (refreshFillLayers), the mask is always the edit target (editingMask), and pixel / position commands refuse them through refuseGroupTarget or pixelTarget.
- Guides are state.guides { vertical, horizontal } (document px) from paint/guides.js; lists are never mutated, so undo entries and .paintproj keep the object. Change them through setGuides(guides, label) in paint.js (pushUndo + redraw). A command that resizes or crops the canvas should move guides with it.

## 2026-10-24 - US-001 (iteration 23: committed)
- The gate is now "no new failures". applyGaussianBlur from the iteration-1 patch was already in the working tree. I reviewed it against the acceptance criteria, re-ran prettier and eslint, and committed it.
- applyGaussianBlur(imgData, radius, mask): sigma = radius clamped 1..32, kernel half-width 3σ, separable H then V passes over premultiplied RGBA, edges clamped. The mask limits writes only. ADJUSTMENTS.gaussianBlur is registered, and 'gaussianBlur' is in the adjust-modal `order` array.
- npm test: 1933 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files. All 5 tests in tests/paint-blur.test.mjs pass.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js (prettier reformat plus menu order), tests/paint-blur.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - adjustments.js and adjust-modal.js are now Prettier-clean, so later diffs on these files will be small.
  - US-002 (sharpen) should call applyGaussianBlur with a small fixed radius on a copy of the ImageData, then blend. Copy `data` first, because the blur works in place.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-002
- applySharpen(imgData, amount, mask): unsharp mask. It blurs a copy of the data with applyGaussianBlur at a fixed radius of 1 (SHARPEN_RADIUS), then sets each RGB channel to orig + (orig - blurred) * amount/100. Alpha isn't touched. Amount 0 returns early. The mask goes to the blur and also gates the writes.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.sharpen ('Sharpen', amount 0..100, default 50) and added 'sharpen' after 'gaussianBlur' in the adjust-modal `order` array.
- tests/paint-sharpen.test.mjs: registry, amount 0, flat image, hard-edge contrast, mask. All 5 pass. npm test: 1938 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-sharpen.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - applyGaussianBlur accepts any `{ width, height, data }` object, so `data.slice()` makes a cheap copy to blur without needing the ImageData constructor (it doesn't exist in node).
  - For edge tests, use mid-gray levels (64/192) instead of 0/255, so the overshoot isn't hidden by clamping.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-003
- applyLevels(imgData, black, gamma, white, mask): builds a 256-entry LUT, t = clamp((v - black) / (white - black)), out = round(t^gamma * 255). RGB only, alpha untouched. black is clamped to 0..254, white to black+1..255, gamma to 0.1..3. With gamma > 1 the midtones darken, as the AC says.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.levels ('Levels': black 0..254 default 0, gamma 0.1..3 step 0.01 default 1, white 1..255 default 255) and added 'levels' after 'brightness' in the adjust-modal `order` array.
- tests/paint-levels.test.mjs: registry, defaults no-op, endpoints and clipping, linear gamma 1, gamma darken/brighten, mask. All 6 pass. npm test: 1944 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-levels.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - adjust-modal already honors `step` on a field (`f.step ?? 1`), so fractional sliders only need `step` in the registry.
  - Per-channel tone maps fit a Uint8ClampedArray(256) LUT. Vibrance (US-004) can't use one because it depends on saturation.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-004
- applyVibrance(imgData, amount, mask) uses HSV saturation s = (max - min) / max and keeps hue and value: each channel becomes max - (max - c) * s'/s. If v > 0, s' = s * (1 + v (1 - s)), so muted colors gain the most and s = 1 stays put. If v < 0, s' = s * (1 + v), which pulls toward gray and reaches full gray at -100. Gray pixels and amount 0 are skipped. Alpha isn't touched.
- This is a simplified version of PhotoCraft's vibrance_px (crates/compose/src/adjust.rs). That one is a fitted, linear-light curve that also damps skin tones. I didn't port the fit.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.vibrance ('Vibrance', amount -100..100, default 0) and added 'vibrance' after 'hsl' in the adjust-modal `order` array.
- tests/paint-vibrance.test.mjs covers the registry, amount 0, gray/black/white, the saturated primary vs a muted color at +100, negative toward gray, and the mask. All 6 pass. npm test: 1950 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-vibrance.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Scaling each channel's distance from max keeps both hue and value, so there's no HSV round trip.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-005
- ellipseMask(x, y, w, h, W, H) in paint/selection.js: a pixel is selected when its center lies inside the ellipse inscribed in the box. Negative w/h are normalized, the loop is clipped to the canvas, and a zero-size box returns an empty mask.
- ellipseSelect tool (TOOLS and TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA, right after rectSelect, icon ◌). Same onDown as rectSelect (tryBeginMove when no modifier, else commit/keep). onMove applies constrainSquare on Shift and previews a dashed ellipse on the overlay. onUp builds the mask and always calls state.applySelectionWithMask(mask, W, H, mode), which handles mode null (replace) and add/subtract/intersect and sets the bbox.
- Added ellipseSelect to the selection-tool lists in pointer.js (move cursor) and paint.js (toolbar and shortcut switches don't commit the selection). No keyboard shortcut; M stays rectSelect.
- tests/paint-ellipse-select.test.mjs: center/corners, circle from a square box (symmetry plus radius bounds), canvas clipping, negative box, registry order, and a drag through the tool with fake ctx/state (Shift circle, subtract modifier, move-on-click). All 7 pass. npm test: 1957 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/selection.js, paint/tools.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-ellipse-select.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - tools.js can import pure helpers from selection.js (selection.js has no imports and no DOM work at load time).
  - Marquee onDown captures `const sel = state.sel` before calling commitSelection, so commitSelection must mutate in place. Test fakes have to do the same, or onUp sees a stale object.
  - New selection tools need adding to SELECTION_TOOLS in pointer.js and to both "don't commit on tool switch" lists in paint.js.
  - Toolbar order is the TOOLS key order (buildToolbar iterates TOOLS).
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-006
- polyLasso tool (TOOLS and TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA, right after lasso, icon ⬠). Each click appends a point. The overlay draws the dashed segments plus a rubber band to the pointer (drawPolyPreview). A click within 6 screen px of the first point closes it, and so do double-click (overlay `dblclick` in paint.js) and Enter. Escape cancels. A repeat click under 1px from the last point is dropped, so a double-click doesn't add a duplicate vertex.
- TOOLS.polyLasso.close(ctx, ov, state) rasterizes with state.scanlineFill (it falls back to the exported scanlineFill) and calls applySelectionWithMask with the mode captured on the first click. With no modifier, commitSelection runs at close rather than on the first click, so Escape (TOOLS.polyLasso.cancel) leaves the old selection untouched. Switching tools mid-polygon cancels it, via setActiveTool.
- Added polyLasso to SELECTION_TOOLS in pointer.js and to both selection-tool lists in paint.js. Added polyPath/polyMode to the initial state. There's no keyboard shortcut.
- tests/paint-poly-lasso.test.mjs (8 tests) covers the right-triangle scanlineFill interior/exterior, registry order, click/preview/close, the double-click duplicate and first-point close, cancel keeping the selection, the add/subtract/intersect modes from the first click, closing with fewer than 3 points, and move-on-click. All pass. npm test: 1965 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/tools.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-poly-lasso.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The freehand lasso sets sel.mode = 'lasso-drawing' even when a modifier is held. applySelectionWithMask only combines when sel.mode === 'active', so lasso add/subtract probably replaces the selection instead. It's a pre-existing bug and I left it alone; it's a candidate for a later fix.
  - drawSelectionOverlay returns early while state.drawing is set for any tool other than rectSelect/lasso, so a tool's own overlay preview isn't overwritten by the marching ants.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-007
- fillLinearGradient(imgData, x0, y0, x1, y1, hex0, hex1, mask) in paint/tools.js projects each pixel center (x+0.5, y+0.5) onto the segment, clamps t to 0..1, writes the rounded RGB mix, and sets alpha to 255. It skips pixels where mask[p] is 0. A zero-length segment returns false and changes nothing.
- Gradient tool (TOOLS.gradient, icon ▤, after fill, with a hint in TOOL_OPTION_SCHEMA). Pointer down records the start. Move draws a dashed black/white preview line on the overlay. Up clears the overlay, then calls state.pushUndo('Gradient') before getImageData/putImageData on the active layer. Shift snaps the angle via constrainLine. Colors are state.color → state.bgColor. A click with no drag does nothing and pushes no undo.
- With an active selection, selectionToFullMask(state.sel, W, H) limits the fill. 'gradient' is in the toolbar's "don't commit selection" list in paint.js, so switching to the tool keeps the selection. It's also in cancelInProgressStroke's strokeTools, so the opposite mouse button cancels the drag.
- If the selection is a float (sel.data/baseData after a paste), the tool clears data/baseData before filling. A later stamp would otherwise restore baseData and erase the gradient.
- tests/paint-gradient.test.mjs (9 tests) covers the endpoints, midpoint, diagonal projection, the mask, zero length, the registry, the tool's preview/undo/write order, the selection limit, and click-without-drag. All pass. npm test: 1974 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/tools.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-gradient.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Switching to a non-selection tool from the toolbar calls commitSelection(), which deselects. Any tool that should respect the selection (gradient, and later the fill/brush clipping or retouch tools) must be added to that list in paint.js.
  - A selection in mode 'active' can still carry a float (data + baseData) after a paste. Before a tool writes pixels under it, clear those fields, or the next stamp/commit overwrites the edit with baseData.
  - Tools that write only on pointer up can call state.pushUndo themselves, instead of joining UNDOABLE_ON_UP in pointer.js. That way a no-op drag doesn't leave an empty undo entry.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-008
- featherMask(mask, W, H, radius) in paint/selection.js: radius is rounded and capped at max(W, H). Radius 0 returns mask.slice(). Otherwise it runs a separable box blur (half-width r, running sums so it's O(W*H) for any radius, and samples past the canvas edge count as unselected), doubles the coverage into a soft ramp, and snaps that back to 0/1 at 0.5 (so coverage >= 0.25 is selected). The original mask is OR'd in, so thin lines and the interior always stay selected. A comment says this wave feathers by expanding the hard mask, because selections are still 0/1.
- PhotoCraft's feather (crates/algo/src/selection.rs) is a Gaussian on a float mask that produces a real soft edge. Paint can't store that yet.
- tests/paint-feather.test.mjs covers radius 0 (copy, not alias), length and 0/1 values, a 10x10 block grown by radius 2 (interior kept, edge neighbors added, far pixels untouched), and radius 500 on an 8x6 canvas. All 4 pass. npm test: 1978 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/selection.js, tests/paint-feather.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - At radius r, the blur-and-threshold grows a straight edge by about r/2 px, and corners come out rounded. US-009 (expand/contract) should do a real dilate/erode instead of reusing this.
  - featherMask isn't wired into the UI yet. US-010 (Select menu) should call it on selectionToFullMask(state.sel, W, H), then hand the result to applySelectionWithMask with mode null, after pushUndo.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-009
- expandMask(mask, W, H, radius) and contractMask(mask, W, H, radius) in paint/selection.js. Radius is rounded and clamped to 0..64, and radius 0 returns mask.slice(). Both use an exact squared Euclidean distance transform (Felzenszwalb–Huttenlocher, one column pass and one row pass, O(W*H) for any radius), the same approach as PhotoCraft's expand/contract (crates/algo/src/selection.rs, `edt`). Expand selects a pixel when d² <= r², which is a disk dilation. Contract is the dual: it keeps a selected pixel only when its distance to the nearest unselected pixel is > r. The canvas edge does not count as unselected, which matches PhotoCraft, so Select All survives a contract.
- tests/paint-selection-morph.test.mjs (7 tests): radius 0 copies, a single pixel expanded by 1 becomes the plus and contracts back to that pixel, the circular footprint at r=5, block edges moving by r with rounded corners, the canvas edge on contract, the clamp at 64, and a 512² r=64 timing check. All pass. npm test: 1985 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/selection.js, tests/paint-selection-morph.test.mjs
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - expandMask/contractMask aren't wired into the UI yet. US-010 should wire them the same way as featherMask: run it on selectionToFullMask(state.sel, W, H), call pushUndo first, then applySelectionWithMask(state, out, W, H, null).
  - Contract on an empty selection, or on a mask with no unselected pixels, returns the input unchanged. The distance stays at the FAR sentinel (1e20), not Infinity, so the parabola math never produces NaN.
---

- Select menu in the header (#select-menu-wrap, after File in the right-side .action-group, so it is nowhere near the top-left nav toggle). Items: Select All, Inverse, a Radius number input (#select-radius, min 0, max 32, default 2), Feather, Expand, Contract. Same toggle / outside-click-close / aria-expanded pattern as the File menu; the handlers live inline in paint.js next to the File menu wiring.
- modifySelection(state, ov, ctx, W, H, op, radius) in paint/selection.js: op is 'feather' | 'expand' | 'contract'. Returns false and changes nothing unless sel.mode is 'active'. Clamps radius to 0..32, stamps a floating paste onto ctx first so its pixels aren't lost, runs the mask op on selectionToFullMask, replaces the selection via applySelectionWithMask(..., null), then redraws the ants (or clears the overlay if contract emptied the selection, since drawSelectionOverlay returns early on mode 'none' without clearing).
- No pushUndo: undo snapshots only layer pixels and objects, not the selection, so an entry would be a misleading no-op. Select All / Inverse don't push undo either.
- .action-menu-field CSS for the label + input row in a menu.

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Undo does not capture state.sel. A selection-only command should not call pushUndo unless undo is taught to snapshot selections.
  - drawSelectionOverlay is a no-op when sel.mode is 'none'; clear `ov` yourself when an operation can empty the selection.

## 2026-10-24 - US-013
- applyPosterize(imgData, levels, mask) in paint/adjustments.js: levels rounded and clamped to 2..255, a 256-entry LUT of round(round(v * (n - 1) / 255) * 255 / (n - 1)) applied to R, G, B; alpha untouched. ADJUSTMENTS.posterize (levels 2..255, step 1, default 4), and 'posterize' after 'threshold' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- 255 levels returns early as a no-op. The PRD formula is not exactly identity at 255: v = 127 gives 127 * 254 / 255 → 127, then 127 * 255 / 254 = 127.5 → 128. The criterion "levels 255 leaves pixels unchanged" wins, since every 8-bit value is already its own level.
- PhotoCraft's own 8-bit rule (crates/compose/src/adjust.rs `posterize`) is different: bin = floor(v * n / 256) capped at n - 1, out = floor(bin * 255 / (n - 1)). That's Photoshop-exact but isn't identity at 255 either. A later story could switch to it if pixel parity with PhotoCraft matters more than the PRD formula.
- tests/paint-posterize.test.mjs (6 tests): registration, levels 2 → 0/255, levels 255 identity, levels 4 matches the formula with alpha preserved, clamp, mask. All pass. npm test: 2002 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Appended US-014 (Emboss filter) since every story now passes.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-posterize.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Check a PRD's pixel formula against its own test criteria before coding; round-half ties can break claimed identities.
  - PhotoCraft's filters live in crates/algo/src/stylize.rs (emboss, find_edges) and its adjustment math is in crates/compose/src/adjust.rs.

## 2026-10-24 - US-016
- Added applyMotionBlur(imgData, angle, distance, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft blur.rs `motion` / `motion_offsets`: |distance| < 0.5 is a no-op; steps = ceil(d), offsets (cos·d·t, -sin·d·t) for t = i / steps - 0.5. The bilinear taps of all offsets are merged once into a fixed kernel (as PhotoCraft does), then applied per pixel over a premultiplied copy with edge clamping; alpha 0 gives RGB 0.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.motionBlur ('Motion Blur', angle 0 / -180..180, distance 10 / 1..200) and added 'motionBlur' after 'gaussianBlur' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-motion-blur.test.mjs (8 tests): registry, flat unchanged, distance < 0.5 no-op, angle 0 stays on its row, angle 90 stays on its column, row sum preserved, transparent neighbor keeps color, mask. npm test: 2020 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so appended US-017 Box Blur (PhotoCraft blur.rs `boxed`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-motion-blur.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - cos(90°) is ~6e-17 in JS, not 0, so a bilinear tap one pixel off-axis gets a ~1e-16 weight. Dropping taps with weight <= 1e-12 keeps angle 90 strictly on its column.
  - Merging taps by "dx,dy" key into a Map gives a small fixed kernel; per-pixel cost is then the tap count, not 4 × samples.

## 2026-10-24 - US-018
- Added applyTwirl(imgData, angle, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft distort.rs `twirl` / `remap`: inscribed-ellipse mapping (cx = hx = W / 2, cy = hy = H / 2), r >= 1 unchanged, t = angle * (1 - r), bilinear sample at the rotated point minus 0.5 from a premultiplied Float32 copy with edge clamping, alpha 0 gives RGB 0. Angle 0 returns early.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.twirl ('Twirl', angle 50 / -999..999) and added 'twirl' after 'findEdges' in buildAdjustMenu's order.

## 2026-10-24 - US-019
- Lifted applyTwirl's premultiplied bilinear sampler into a shared `remapEllipse(imgData, mask, warp)` in paint/adjustments.js (twirl behaviour unchanged, its 7 tests still pass), and added applyPinch(imgData, amount, mask) on top of it following PhotoCraft distort.rs `pinch`: a = clamp(amount / 100, -1, 1), r <= 0 or r >= 1 unchanged, k = r^(1 - a/2) / r. Amount 0 returns early.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.pinch ('Pinch', amount 50 / -100..100) and added 'pinch' after 'twirl' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-pinch.test.mjs (7 tests): registry, amount 0, flat image, corners, odd-size centre at +/-100, dot direction, mask. npm test: 2041 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- The story's test criterion had the dot direction backwards. In PhotoCraft's formula a positive amount gives k > 1, so it samples farther out and *shrinks* a centred dot, the way Photoshop's Pinch does. I kept the formula, tested the real direction, and fixed the criterion and notes in prd.json.
- All stories passed, so appended US-020 Spherize (Normal mode) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-pinch.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Before you write a direction test for a radial remap, work out whether k = source radius / output radius is above or below 1. If k > 1 the centre shrinks; if k < 1 it is magnified. Story notes can get this wrong.
  - Prettier keeps `remapEllipse`'s comma-chained const declarations as they are, so the style matches the older filters.

## 2026-10-24 - US-020
- Added applySpherize(imgData, amount, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft distort.rs `spherize` (Normal) / `sphere_1d`: a = clamp(amount / 100, -1, 1), r <= 0 or r >= 1 unchanged, target = asin(r) * 2 / PI for a >= 0 else sin(r * PI / 2), k = (r + |a| * (target - r)) / r. It goes through the shared remapEllipse sampler. Amount 0 returns early.

## 2026-10-24 - US-021
- Added applyMedian(imgData, radius, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft noise.rs `median` (no threshold): r = round(max(0, radius)), r = 0 no-op, disc dx^2 + dy^2 <= r^2 + r, each RGBA channel takes the sample at index floor(count / 2), read from a copy, outside the image reads as 0. A per-channel 256-bin histogram (one Int32Array(1024)) is rebuilt per row and slid per pixel by removing/adding the two ends of each disc row; the median is a cumulative scan of the bins. Radius 10 on 256x256 runs in ~60 ms in node.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.median ('Median', radius 1 / 1..100) and added 'median' after 'boxBlur' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-median.test.mjs (9 tests): registry, radius 0, lone speck, flat interior, sharp edge at r = 2, 3x3 window via diagonals (5 white vs 4), exact match against a brute-force sort at r = 1, 2, 3, 5 (borders included), speed bound, mask. npm test: 2057 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so appended US-022 Dust & Scratches (median with threshold) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-median.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - A brute-force reference implementation inside the test, run on a seeded LCG noise image, is the cheapest way to prove a fast windowed filter exact, borders included.
  - One histogram path works for r = 1 too; PhotoCraft's median_direct split is only a speed choice, and the output is identical.

## 2026-10-24 - US-023

## 2026-10-24 - US-026
- Added applySmartSharpen(imgData, amount, radius, reduceNoise, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft sharpen.rs `smart` (with_blur): applyGaussianBlur on an unmasked copy at radius, k = amount / 100, nr = clamp(reduceNoise / 100, 0, 1), and for each R, G, B of every alpha > 0 pixel d = orig - blurred, keep = 1 - nr * exp(-(|d| / 255) * 40), out = clamp(round(orig + k * d * keep)). Alpha unchanged; alpha-0 pixels skipped; mask limits writes only.

## 2026-10-24 - US-032
- Added applyPolarCoordinates(imgData, mode, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft distort.rs `polar` / `polar_map` with the image as the bounds: the map runs at the integer pixel coordinate (X - 0.5, Y - 0.5) and writes (sx + 0.5, sy + 0.5). Mode 0 (Rect → Polar): theta = atan2(-dx, -dy), + 2π when <= 0, source (theta / 2π * W, r / rmax * H). Mode 1 (Polar → Rect): theta = (x + 1) / W * 2π, r = y / H * rmax, source (cx - r sin theta, cy - r cos theta). Sampling goes through remapPixels (Edge::Repeat).
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.polarCoordinates ('Polar Coordinates', mode 0..1 = 0) and added 'polarCoordinates' after 'ripple' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-polar.test.mjs (7 tests) on a non-square 12x8 chart, distinct in every channel: registry + apply dispatch, flat no-op in both modes, hand-computed pixels for both modes (incl. the 12 o'clock seam reading column W - 1), a corner outside the circle checked against a hand bilinear mix of the repeated bottom row, mode rounding/clamping, mask. npm test: 2136 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so appended US-033 Wave (PhotoCraft distort.rs `wave` / `shape`, needs Wrap / Transparent edges on remapPixels) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-polar.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - JS atan2 keeps signed zeros like Rust: atan2(-0, positive) is -0 (so `<= 0` catches the seam) and atan2(-0, negative) is -π. Test pixels exactly on the 12 / 6 o'clock axes exercise both.
  - Use a non-square test image for any filter that mixes W, H, and min(W, H); a square one hides swaps.

## 2026-10-24 - US-037
- Added applyDiffuse(imgData, mode, seed, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft stylize2.rs `diffuse` (RGB with alpha): mode rounds/clamps 0..3 (Normal, Darken Only, Lighten Only, Anisotropic), seed rounds/clamps 0..9999. Modes 0..2 copy the hash01-chosen neighbour (dx, dy in -1..1); Darken / Lighten copy only on lower / higher luma and skip an alpha-0 neighbour of a pixel with alpha > 0. Anisotropic walks DIFFUSE_RING from a hashed start, keeps the first strictly closest luma, and rounds the 4-channel average. Out-of-image reads are [0, 0, 0, 0]; reads come from a copy.

## 2026-10-24 - US-038
- Added applyWind(imgData, method, direction, seed, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft stylize2.rs `wind_direct` (RGB with alpha): method rounds/clamps 0..2 (Wind, Blast, Stagger), direction 0..1 (From Right d = -1, From Left d = +1), seed 0..9999. Stagger copies the source shifted by a hashed per-segment amount; Wind / Blast (strength 4 / 8, lmax 16 / 40) blend toward the upwind source with the strictly largest edge * (1 - k / (len + 1)) weight. Out-of-image reads are [0, 0, 0, 0]; reads come from a copy.

## 2026-10-24 - US-039
- Added applySurfaceBlur(imgData, radius, threshold, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft blur.rs `surface_direct` (RGB with alpha): r = round(radius) clamped 1..100, t = threshold clamped 2..255 * 2.5. Each R, G, B is the max(0, 1 - |v - v0| / t)-weighted mean of the window, skipping outside and alpha-0 pixels; a zero weight sum keeps v0. Alpha is untouched. A per-channel 256-bin histogram slides along each row (surface_8bit) and only visits bins within ceil(t) of v0. Reads come from a copy.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.surfaceBlur ('Surface Blur', radius 1..100 = 5, threshold 2..255 = 15) and ADJUSTMENTS.despeckle ('Despeckle', no fields, radius 2 threshold 12). Added 'surfaceBlur' after 'boxBlur' and 'despeckle' after 'dustScratches' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-surface-blur.test.mjs (10 tests): registry + apply dispatch for both, flat unchanged, step edge stays sharp while noise is smoothed, a hand-computed border pixel with an alpha-0 neighbour, equality with a direct window sum on noisy data with transparent pixels, alpha kept, radius/threshold rounding and clamping, despeckle == (2, 12), radius 100 on 64x48 timing, mask. npm test: 2197 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so appended US-040 (Blur, Blur More, Sharpen More, Sharpen Edges) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-surface-blur.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Weighted means with weights like 1 - d / t accumulate float error, so a true .5 tie can round down (116.49999 instead of 116.5). Scale the weights by 2t (2t - 2d) and whole-threshold sums stay exact integers. Write the test's direct reference the same way, or it disagrees on ties.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-040
- applyGaussianBlur now follows PhotoCraft blur.rs gaussian_kernel below sigma 1: sigma = min(32, radius), and sigma < 0.05 (or 0 / negative / NaN) returns without touching pixels. The Gaussian Blur dialog is a range input with min 1, so it never reaches sub-1 sigmas; only presets do.
- Added applyConvolve3(imgData, kernel, scale, offset, mask) following PhotoCraft other2.rs `custom` (3x3 core): straight (not premultiplied) RGB, sum(k * v) / scale + offset rounded and clamped, scale 0 counts as 1, edges repeat, alpha kept, reads from a copy.
- Registered blur (Gaussian 0.6), blurMore ([1 2 1; 2 2 2; 1 2 1] / 14), sharpenMore (Unsharp 150 / 0.6 / 0), sharpenEdges (Unsharp 100 / 0.8 / 6), all with fields: []. Menu order: blur, blurMore before gaussianBlur; sharpenMore, sharpenEdges after sharpen.
- tests/paint-blur-presets.test.mjs (13 tests) on 9x7. npm test: 2210 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so appended US-041 De-Interlace (PhotoCraft video.rs `deinterlace`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-blur-presets.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - At sigma 0.05 the Gaussian kernel is r = 1 but its side taps are exp(-200), so it rounds to the identity on bytes. Use sigma >= ~0.3 in a test that needs a visible blur.
  - A linear ramp is a fixed point of a symmetric blur (except a sub-0.5 drift at the clamped border), so unsharp masking leaves it unchanged even with threshold 0. Add a bump to show sharpening.

## 2026-10-24 - US-042
- Added applyNtscColors(imgData, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft video.rs `ntsc` for RGB: YIQ on 0..1 channels; chroma <= 1e-7 or k >= 1 leaves the pixel alone; otherwise I and Q scale by k so Y +/- chroma stays inside (-20 - 7.5) / 92.5 .. (120 - 7.5) / 92.5, then the YIQ inverse writes R, G, B (Uint8ClampedArray rounds and clamps). Alpha untouched, alpha-0 processed, mask limits writes.

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Most fully saturated primaries are NTSC-safe: magenta, pure green, red, orange and 0,0,220 blue pass unchanged. Only yellow, cyan, near-pure blue and similar exceed the limits. Check a fixture actually needs correction before asserting it changes.

## 2026-10-24 - US-044
- Added applyFragment(imgData, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `fragment`: each pixel is the mean of the samples at (x -/+ 4, y) and (x, y -/+ 4), with R, G, B weighted by alpha, alpha the plain mean, and an alpha sum of 0 giving transparent black. Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes.
- Edge rule: off-canvas samples repeat the edge pixel. They are not transparent. `Image::get` returns 0 outside its rect, but the engine (crates/engine/src/filters.rs) calls algo::apply_in, which builds src with Image::read_clamped to canvas ∪ content. For a canvas-sized paint layer, that means clamping to the image. The test asserts this. The story's "edge picks up transparency" criterion is replaced by it, as the story notes allowed.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.fragment ('Fragment', fields: []) and added 'fragment' after 'pixelate' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-fragment.test.mjs (7 tests) on 11x9. They cover registry + dispatch, flat interior unchanged, a hand-computed interior pixel, alpha weighting with a half-transparent and an alpha-0 neighbour, all alpha-0 neighbours giving transparent black, edge clamping, and the mask. npm test: 2238 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so I appended US-045 Facet (PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `facet_direct`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-fragment.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Every algo neighbourhood filter reaches paint through algo::apply_in, so edges repeat. Image::get's 0-outside only shows up in algo unit tests. Use edge clamping unless the filter reads beyond its halo on purpose.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-045
- Added applyFacet(imgData, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `facet_direct`: a radius-2 Kuwahara filter. Each pixel takes the plain mean (alpha included) of whichever 3x3 quadrant, at offsets (-2,-2), (0,-2), (-2,0), (0,0), has the lowest luma variance (0.299/0.587/0.114, mean(l^2) - mean(l)^2). Strict less-than keeps the earliest quadrant on ties. Off-canvas samples repeat the edge pixel. Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.facet ('Facet', fields: []) and added 'facet' after 'fragment' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-facet.test.mjs (7 tests) on 7x6: registry + dispatch, flat unchanged, flat quadrant beating edge-straddling ones, plain (not premultiplied) averaging with a transparent-black sample, the earliest-quadrant tie rule, edge repetition at corners and the left edge, and the mask. npm test: 2245 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so I appended US-046 Crystallize (PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `crystallize_direct`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-facet.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Luma ignores alpha, so pixels with the same RGB and different alpha give zero-variance quadrants. That's an easy way to build exact ties and hand-checkable alpha means in Kuwahara-style tests.
  - Every Facet quadrant contains the target pixel, and a corner pixel's outward quadrant is nine copies of itself. That's why corners never change.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-047
- Added applyPointillize(imgData, cellSize, seed, background, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `pointillize_direct` (origin 0, 0), plus an exported pointillizeDot(x, y, cell, seed) helper that returns [depth, gx, gy, px, py] or null. It reuses crystallizeCellPoint and hash01. Radius, distance and depth are in f32 via Math.fround. The strict depth > best rule keeps the first cell in dy-then-dx order. The dot colour is the source at floor(dot centre), clamped to the canvas, with RGB jittered by (hash01(gx, gy, 17) - 0.5) * 0.08 * 255. It blends over the background at k = clamp(depth + 0.5, 0, 1); canvas alpha is max(source alpha, background alpha). Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes.

  - The adjust dialog only renders range inputs. A filter with a colour parameter gets a fixed default until a colour field exists.

## 2026-10-24 - US-048
- Added applyMezzotint(imgData, type, seed, mask) and an exported mezzotintThreshold(type, x, y, k, seed) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `mezzotint` / `mezzo_threshold`. Type rounds and clamps to 0..9, and seed to 0..9999. Each RGB channel becomes 255 when fround(value / 255) > threshold, else 0. Alpha is untouched. It reuses hash01, with Math.floor for div_euclid and Math.trunc on an fround'ed phase for Rust's `as i32`. Grainy Dots mixes in f32.

- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-mezzotint.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The Lines/Strokes phase hashes include the channel (k + 40, k + 50), so R, G and B each have their own segment boundaries. A row segment is constant per channel, not per pixel.
  - Compare against fround(value / 255). A double can land on the other side of an f32 threshold.

## 2026-10-24 - US-049
- Added applyColorHalftone(imgData, maxRadius, angle1, angle2, angle3, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft pixelate.rs `color_halftone` for RGB at origin 0, 0. maxRadius clamps 4..127 (default 8) and angles clamp -360..360 (defaults 108/162/90). cell = rmax * SQRT2. Each channel rotates the pixel centre into screen space, visits the 3x3 cells (skipping d >= rmax + 0.5), averages the five clamped taps at the cell centre, maps ink to a radius (area-true up to PI/4, then grows to cover the corners), and takes the max of min(clamp(r - d + 0.5), PI r^2). Output = round((1 - cov) * 255). Alpha is untouched. Everything is in f32 via Math.fround. Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.colorHalftone ('Color Halftone', maxRadius / channel1 / channel2 / channel3) and added it after 'mezzotint' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-color-halftone.test.mjs (8 tests): registry + dispatch, white unchanged with alpha kept, black covered inside each dot and partial at cell corners, a hand-checked 191-grey dot (r = 3.19776, pixel (8, 5) gives 38), angle 0 against an independent axis-aligned grid formula plus exact transpose symmetry, five-tap clamping with a black column 0 (pixel (1, 2) gives 214), maxRadius / angle / default clamping, and the mask. npm test: 2280 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so I appended US-050 Clouds and Difference Clouds (PhotoCraft render.rs `clouds`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-color-halftone.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The halftone cell (rmax * SQRT2) is never an integer, so there's no exact pixel period to assert. Compare against an independent grid formula with a 1-level tolerance, and use transpose symmetry at angle 0 for an exact check.
  - PhotoCraft's screen.rs is only a per-cell radius cache. The direct per-pixel computation is bit-identical to its reference.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-050
- Added applyClouds(imgData, seed, difference, mask) to paint/adjustments.js, plus exported cloudHash(x, y, seed) and cloudsValue(x, y, base, seed). They follow PhotoCraft render.rs `clouds` / `clouds_value` / `cloud_hash` at origin 0, 0, black to white. seed rounds and clamps to 0..9999, and base = clamp(fround(max(W, H) / 4), 16, 512). The hash is u32 via Math.imul and >>> 0, so negative coordinates wrap like Rust's `as u32`. The fBm (8 octaves, seed + o * 7919) is f32 via Math.fround. Clouds writes round(fround(t * 255)) to RGB and sets alpha 255. Difference Clouds writes round(|fround(v / 255) - t| * 255) and keeps alpha. The mask limits writes.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.clouds ('Clouds') and ADJUSTMENTS.differenceClouds ('Difference Clouds'), each with a seed field 0..9999 = 0, and added both after 'colorHalftone' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-clouds.test.mjs (8 tests) on 13x9. They cover registry + dispatch; cloudHash against hand-mixed u32 values, including (-1, 2, 3) -> 0x2ade0f1b; cloudsValue at the origin as a sum of corner hashes (seed 2 gives 0.61825, seed 0 clamps to 0); interpolated values (5, 3) and (9, 4) from an independent Python f32 reference; grey/opaque output; base 16 for 20x10 and base 25 for 100x4; seed determinism, difference, and clamping; difference on black/white/mixed; and the mask. npm test: 2288 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so I appended US-051 Fibers (PhotoCraft render.rs `fibers`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-clouds.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - At (0, 0) every noise octave reads its lattice corner with zero interpolation weight, which makes a clean hand check. With seed 0, Clouds is clamped to 0 there, so use seed 2.
  - cloud_hash is not hash01. It uses its own 13 / 0x5bd1e995 / 15 mix and divides by 0xffffff. Fibers' value_noise does use hash01(a, b, 0, seed).

## 2026-10-24 - US-054
- Added applyRelight(imgData, params, mask) to paint/adjustments.js following PhotoCraft relight.rs `relight` / `relight_rect` with the canvas as bounds at 0, 0, plus exported relightSigma / relightStep. The packed (luma, weight) field covers the canvas inflated by read_radius (3 sigma + 1 + step) with edge-clamped reads, alpha-0 pixels get weight 0, and the field goes through the engine's three-box Gaussian. s0 = max(shading, 0.04), finite-difference normal at the step, light_dir / light_color(warmth) as in the engine, out = (c / s0) * shade * lightColor. Alpha kept, alpha-0 skipped, mask limits writes, f32 via Math.fround. Engine clamps for all six params; intensity 0 is the identity.
- Factored the running-sum pass out of applyBoxBlur into boxPass(from, to, n, r, start, step, len), and added gaussBoxRadii (fxutil.rs `gauss_box_radii`, Kovesi, f32) and gaussBlurN (fxutil.rs `gauss_blur_n`, three row passes then three column passes, f32 storage between passes). Box Blur output is unchanged.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.relight ('Relight') and added 'relight' after 'lightingEffects' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-relight.test.mjs (9 tests) on 40x20: registry + dispatch; identity at intensity 0 (and negative); a flat [100, 150, 200] pixel -> [144, 216, 255] for every angle; a symmetric ridge where angle 180 brightens the left slope and angle 0 the right, mirrored exactly; warmth +100 gives R > G > B and -100 gives B > R; alpha kept, an alpha-0 pixel untouched and held out of its neighbours' shading; sigma / step clamps and gaussBoxRadii for sigma 0.4 / 2 / 20; parameter clamping; the mask. npm test: 2324 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.

  - PhotoCraft's Gaussian-of-boxes helper is now gaussBlurN(buf, w, h, n, sigma) in paint/adjustments.js. Use it for any engine call to fxutil `gauss_blur_n`; keep the buffer a Float32Array so each pass rounds to f32.

## 2026-10-24 - US-055
- Added radialIntervals(pathLength, quality) (blur.rs `radial_intervals`: ceil clamped to 1..64 / 256 / 4096, NaN gives 1) and applyRadialBlur(imgData, params, mask) following blur.rs `radial` / `average_samples` and the blur/radial.rs fast path, with the canvas as bounds at 0, 0. Centre (W * centerX, H * centerY) from percents; Spin uses n + 1 samples over an arc of amount degrees, and Zoom uses k = 1 - span * i / n with span = amount / 200. Each sample is bilinear in premultiplied alpha, unpremultiplied and then re-premultiplied as the engine does. The average is unpremultiplied with an alpha > 1e-7 guard. f32 via Math.fround, with per-n sample tables cached. Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes.
- Differs from the story text: off-canvas bilinear taps are transparent, not clamped. PhotoCraft's radial path calls Image::sample with Edge::Transparent, and blur/radial.rs `Source::pixel` skips out-of-rect taps. Spin therefore lowers alpha at the corners, where samples rotate off the canvas, while colour stays put. Zoom never leaves the canvas.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.radialBlur ('Radial Blur': amount, method, quality, centerX, centerY) and added 'radialBlur' after 'motionBlur' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-radial-blur.test.mjs (8 tests) on a 9x7 pattern with R = 30x, G = 35y, B = 100, and A = 128 where (x + y) % 3 == 0, else 255. They cover registry + dispatch, radialIntervals caps / minimum / NaN, the unchanged centre pixel and a flat image (Zoom exact, Spin keeps colour), and four hand-checked pixels from an independent Python double-precision reference: Spin 30 (7, 1) -> [210, 37, 100, 213], Spin 100 (0, 0) -> [4, 33, 100, 36], Zoom 40 (8, 6) -> [230, 203, 100, 225], and Zoom 60 at (25%, 75%) (1, 5) -> [36, 173, 100, 145]. They also check that Spin keeps rings and Zoom keeps rays on 41x41, premultiplied averaging next to transparency, clamping (including Draft != Good on 120x120), and the mask. npm test: 2332 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- All stories passed, so I appended US-056 Smart Blur (PhotoCraft blur2.rs `smart_blur`) to prd.json.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-radial-blur.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Halo::Bounds filters (radial, twirl, wave, offset ...) read the whole layer with Image::read, not read_clamped, so check each sampler's Edge argument rather than assuming edge repeat.
  - Engine sampling is Image::sample (pixel centres at .5, premultiplied taps, straight output); fxutil `add_sample_premul` clamps but only some filters use it.

## 2026-10-24 - US-056
