- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - smart_blur's doc says off-window neighbours read as zero, but the layer path's halo is ceil(r) + 1 through read_clamped, so on the canvas every read repeats the edge. Read the halo_ext entry in crates/algo/src/lib.rs, not the kernel's doc comment.
  - On a non-dividing stride (Medium at r = 100 is stride 15) the grid runs -100..95 and is asymmetric, and the dy = -100 row has no offsets. Don't assume the first offset sits on the top row.
  - Adjust dialog sliders default to step 1, so fractional-min fields (0.1..100) need `step: 0.1` or the slider can only reach 0.1, 1.1, ...
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-057
- Added shapeBlurSpans(r, inside) (blur2.rs `Spans::new`: inclusive [dy, dx0, dx1] runs over -ceil(r)..ceil(r), with r < 0.5 and empty kernels falling back to the centre pixel), SHAPE_BLUR_INSIDE (all nine `shape_inside` predicates in f32, with polygonRadius for the hexagon and the even-odd inPoly star at inner radius 0.42), and applyShapeBlur(imgData, params, mask) following shape_blur.rs `Prepared` with the canvas as bounds at 0, 0. Per-row f64 prefix sums run over premultiplied f32 RGBA, padded by ceil(r) + 1 repeated edge pixels (the layer path reads through read_clamped with that halo), so each span is two lookups. The mean divides by the fixed kernel count and unpremultiplies with the alpha > 1e-7 guard. radius clamps 5..1000 (default 10); shape rounds and clamps 0..8. Reads come from a copy; the mask limits writes. A radius-1000 star on 50x40 takes about 0.5 s.
- Registered ADJUSTMENTS.shapeBlur ('Shape Blur': radius, shape) and added 'shapeBlur' after 'smartBlur' in buildAdjustMenu's order.
- tests/paint-shape-blur.test.mjs (8 tests): registry, dispatch, and menu order; [count, runs] per shape at r = 8 from an independent Python double reference, plus PhotoCraft's own r = 3 table; the r < 0.5 and empty-kernel fallbacks; flat unchanged for every shape; hand-checked r = 5 pixels on a 9x7 pattern (Circle (4, 3) -> [101, 90, 100, 214] and corner (0, 0) -> [30, 36, 100, 191], Star (4, 3) / (8, 6), Heart (2, 1)); premultiplied averaging next to transparency (46 / 81 alpha, no red bleed); clamping and rounding, including 5000 -> 1000; the mask. npm test: 2349 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- prd.json was re-prioritised during this iteration: US-057 moved to priority 70, behind new layout stories US-058..US-062. The prompt says to finish the story in progress first, so I finished US-057. The next iteration should take US-058 (menu bar).
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-shape-blur.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - I checked span counts against a scratch rustc build of blur2.rs's Spans / shape_inside in /tmp, outside both repos. JS matched it exactly for every shape at r = 3..1000, except one hexagon tie at (-r, 0). Native macOS atan2f(0, -1) returns the f32 below pi; JS and musl/wasm return pi rounded up. The JS keeps the wasm behaviour (+1 pixel).
  - A double-precision reference disagrees with the f32 engine on exact-boundary ties (Triangle at r = 5: 41 in double, 43 in f32 and in the engine). Pick test radii without ties, or verify ties in f32.

## 2026-10-24 - US-063
- Added the Clone Stamp tool (TOOLS.cloneStamp, icon ⎘, after Eyedropper in the tool column). Alt+click sets state.clone.source (pixel under the cursor). A drag with no source draws "Alt-click to set a clone source" on the overlay and changes nothing (no undo). With a source, onDown pushes undo('Clone Stamp'), drops a float's data/baseData (as gradient does), snapshots the layer, and lays dabs at quarter-radius spacing; each move puts back only the touched box.
- New paint/retouch.js: tipShape / tipReach / dabCoverage port PhotoCraft's round tip (lib.rs TIP_SHAPE, soft_exp). cloneStrokeOffset(clone, x, y, aligned) returns the whole-pixel offset: Aligned keeps the first stroke's offset, unaligned restarts from the source every stroke. cloneDab(dst, src, W, H, cx, cy, dx, dy, radius, hardness, opacity, mask, cov) composites src[p + offset] over src[p] (straight-alpha over_native) at k = cov × opacity; cov builds up c + d(1 - c), like PhotoCraft stroke_coverage + apply_coverage. Off-canvas source pixels and mask-0 pixels are untouched.
- Options: Size (default 20, diameter), Hardness 80, Opacity 100, Aligned on, plus a hint. Clone Stamp is in the toolbar's keep-selection list and in cancelInProgressStroke (undoes only when the stroke actually pushed undo).
- tests/paint-clone-stamp.test.mjs (12 tests): tip coverage, offset copy, no re-sampling within a stroke, no opacity compounding, off-canvas source, mask, Aligned on/off (pure and through the tool), the no-source hint, undo before put, and the selection limit. npm test: 2385 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Appended US-064 (Dodge and Burn), the next retouch brush.
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js (new), paint/tools.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-clone-stamp.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Use Math.floor for both the Alt-click source and the stroke start: with Math.round, a stroke starting at a pixel centre (x.5) rounds up and lands one pixel off the source.
  - A hard tip (hardness 100) still has a ~0.9-1.2 px soft edge (core = R - edge), so pixel-exact test assertions must sit well inside the radius.
  - No clone-source crosshair or Clone Source panel yet; the hint lives in the options bar and on the overlay.

## 2026-10-24 - US-064
- Added Dodge (◐) and Burn (✋) tools after Clone Stamp, both built by toneTool(burn) in paint/tools.js. Options from toneSchema(burn): Size 40, Hardness 50, Range select (Shadows / Midtones / Highlights, default Midtones), Exposure 1-100 (50), Protect Tones (on), and a hint.
- paint/retouch.js: toneCurve(v, e, range, burn), dodgeBurn(rgb, e, range, burn, protectTones) (with PhotoCraft's luma-preserving compressGamut), and toneDab(dst, src, W, H, cx, cy, radius, hardness, tone, mask, cov). That ports tone_stroke: each pixel is toned once from src (pre-stroke) and mixed in by the stroke's coverage, which builds up c + d(1 - c) to 1. A stroke never goes past its Exposure, a new stroke builds on the last, and alpha is never written.
- Pulled the dab walk out of cloneStampSegment into dabSegment(x0, y0, x1, y1, step, dab) + putBox(ctx, img, box). Clone Stamp and toneSegment both use it.
- paint.js: dodge / burn are in the cancel stroke set and pushedOnDown, and in the toolbar's keep-selection list. cancelInProgressStroke also clears state.toneStroke.
- tests/paint-dodge-burn.test.mjs (9 tests): midtone weighting, burn, Shadows / Highlights weighting, Protect Tones hue, no compounding (pure and through the tool), mask + alpha, the schema, undo before put, the selection limit. npm test: 2394 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Appended US-065 (Sponge), the next retouch brush.
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-dodge-burn.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - No keyboard shortcut: PhotoCraft's O is already Ellipse in paint.js. Leave shortcuts alone unless a story asks for them.
  - PhotoCraft tones in straight 0..1 RGB with no linearization; dividing the bytes by 255 matches it.
  - Sponge, Blur, Sharpen, and Smudge are sequential in PhotoCraft (color_dab with per_dab(e, spacing)): they edit the working pixels, unlike tone_stroke's accumulate-from-original.

## 2026-10-24 - US-070
- The arrow tool (id still `select`, V shortcut unchanged) is now PhotoCraft's Move: label Move, icon ✥, cursor move. Pressing inside an active or floating selection calls tryBeginMove and moves the selected pixels. Pressing a text or image object moves it (corner handles still resize, double-click text still re-edits). Pressing anywhere else starts a `mode: 'layer'` drag that offsets the active layer through the new pure `offsetLayerPixels(src, W, H, dx, dy, fill)` in paint/tools.js. On release it puts the pre-move pixels back, calls pushUndo('Move Layer'), and re-applies the offset, so the move is one undo step. Layer 0 fills the vacated area with the background colour and other layers with transparency (same as liftSelection).
- Removed the Google Drawings behavior: the empty-space rubber band (objectsIntersectingRect, the marquee drag/overlay/status code), Shift-click add/remove of objects, and click-empty-to-lift-ink (liftDrawingAt plus the now-dead floodSelectInk). Clicking an object always selects only that object.
- tests/paint-move-tool.test.mjs (5 tests): the label is Move and the hint has no Google, marquee, or Shift+click text; offsetLayerPixels handles right/down, left/up with rounding and a fill colour, zero offset, and an off-canvas offset; source checks that the layer mode, the selection move, and Move Layer undo are present and that the marquee and additive paths are gone. npm test: 2417 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Files changed: paint/tools.js, paint/paint.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/objects.js, paint/history.js, tests/paint-move-tool.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Object multi-selection can still come back through undo entries (selectedObjectIds is an array), so the delete and status code still handle more than one id. Nothing in the UI creates a multi-selection now.
  - The Move tool's floating-selection move goes through the global `sel.mode === 'moving'` path in pointer.js. beginObjectSelect only has to call tryBeginMove and return.

## 2026-10-24 - US-065
- Reapplied the saved Sponge patch (/tmp/ralph-US-065-sponge.patch) to paint/retouch.js and paint/tools.js. retouch.js adds saturation, sponge (PhotoCraft sponge: move toward/away from Rec. 601 luma, gamut-compressed around luma, Vibrance damping), perDab (1 - (1 - e)^spacing), and colorDab (sequential dabs over a Float32Array RGB working copy). TOOLS.sponge (icon ◍) snapshots the layer on down, pushes undo 'Sponge', and writes RGB back through putBox with alpha left alone. Strength is flow * 0.5 per PhotoCraft ("a full-flow pass moves colours half-way"). The schema has Size, Hardness, Mode (Desaturate default / Saturate), Flow (1-100, 50), Vibrance (on).
- sponge added to DRAG_STROKE_TOOLS (paint/pointer.js) and the keep-selection list in paint/paint.js. The patch's cancelInProgressStroke hunks were dropped; that function is gone since US-069.
- tests/paint-sponge.test.mjs (9 tests). npm test: 2430 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Every story passed after this, so US-072 (Blur and Sharpen tools) was appended as the next retouch-brush gap.
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/paint.js, paint/pointer.js, tests/paint-sponge.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft is f32 and JS is f64: sponge on a grey or a gamut-edge colour drifts by ~1e-16, so compare with a tolerance rather than deepEqual.
  - A colour already at the gamut edge (e.g. [1, 0.1, 0.1]) barely moves under Saturate with or without Vibrance. Test Vibrance's damping on a colour inside the gamut, e.g. [0.8, 0.2, 0.2].
  - Sequential-dab tools (Sponge, later Blur / Sharpen / Smudge) use colorDab-style in-order edits with perDab, not the toneDab coverage cap that Dodge / Burn use.
---

## 2026-10-24 - US-072
- Blur and Sharpen tools. paint/retouch.js adds localBlur (PhotoCraft local_blur: separable Gaussian over a window, r = ceil(3σ), edge clamped, colour premultiplied by alpha, original colour kept where blurred alpha ≈ 0), localSharpen (v + amount·(v − blur₁(v)), clamped to the 3x3 range with Protect Detail, alpha unchanged), and focusDab (filter the dab box of the working copy, then move each covered pixel toward the result by perDab(coverage × strength, spacing)).
- paint/tools.js: focusSchema / focusTool factories give TOOLS.blur (💧) and TOOLS.sharpen (△) with Size, Hardness, Strength (1-100, 50) and Sharpen's Protect Detail (on). sigma = clamp(size × 0.03, 1, 4), sharpen amount 1, strength = Strength / 100 (no 0.5 factor, unlike Sponge). Undo is pushed on down. Sponge's write-back loop became putWork(ctx, img, work, n, box), shared with focusSegment (n = 4, so Blur's alpha changes are written back).
- blur and sharpen joined DRAG_STROKE_TOOLS (paint/pointer.js) and the keep-selection list (paint/paint.js).
- tests/paint-blur-sharpen-tool.test.mjs (7 tests). npm test: 2437 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Every story passed after this, so US-073 (Smudge tool) was appended.
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-blur-sharpen-tool.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft's Focus dab blends every channel, alpha included, toward the filtered window, so Blur can soften alpha edges; Sharpen's filter returns alpha unchanged.
  - The filter reads from the same working copy the dabs write, so later dabs in a stroke blur already-blurred pixels (PhotoCraft does the same).

## 2026-10-24 - US-073
- Smudge tool. paint/retouch.js adds mixPremultiplied(dst, src, k, di, si) (PhotoCraft mix_premultiplied: premultiplied lerp, straight mix where the result alpha is ~0), smudgeStroke(strength, finger, maxSize) (carry buffer of (2r+1)^2 premultiplied pixels, r = ceil(size / 2) + 2, filled lazily by the first dab from the clamped pixels under it or the finger colour), and smudgeDab (lay down at coverage x strength, then carry = carry*strength + pixel*(1 - strength) over the whole square). The lay-down has no perDab; PhotoCraft's Smudge uses the raw coverage.
- paint/tools.js: TOOLS.smudge (icon a pointing finger) with Size, Hardness, Strength (1-100, 50), Finger Painting (off, uses state.color). Undo 'Smudge' is pushed on down. smudgeSegment writes back through putWork(..., 4, box). focusTool's work-copy loop became rgbaWork(img), shared with Smudge.
- smudge joined DRAG_STROKE_TOOLS (paint/pointer.js) and the keep-selection list (paint/paint.js).
- tests/paint-smudge.test.mjs (8 tests). npm test: 2445 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Every story passed after this, so US-074 (Healing Brush) was appended.
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-smudge.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The mask limits Smudge's lay-down, not its pickup, so colour from outside the selection can still be carried in (PhotoCraft composites the selection afterwards; same visible result for the masked pixels).
  - Smudging from transparency into an opaque area thins alpha but leaves RGB alone (the premultiplied carry is 0); test RGB on pixels with alpha > 0, and expect some alpha < 255.
  - PhotoCraft's Healing Brush heals the whole stroke in one Poisson solve (heal_region) once coverage is known, not per dab; crates/algo/src/poisson.rs is SOR with a cascadic half-resolution start.

## 2026-10-24 - US-077
- Spot Healing Brush (Proximity Match). paint/retouch.js adds dilateMask (square dilation), spotSamplingWindow (content_aware::sampling_window), bestOffset (inpaint::best_offset: summed-area table of the domain, ring sample points subsampled to ~2000, coarse grid step max(1, trunc(margin / 24)) then full-res refinement, early cutoff every 32 points, 1e-3 distance penalty), proximitySourcePatch (copies from the pre-heal pixels, null off the grid), membraneFill (solveMembrane per channel), spotHealStroke (spot_heal_surface: domain = coverage dilated 2, heal mask dilated 1, ring = trunc(clamp(size / 8, 3, 16)), margin = floor(max(w, h) / 2), seamlessClone over the heal mask bounds + 1, composite at the coverage), and coverageDab. healStroke's bounds and composite moved into shared coverBounds / healOver.

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The sampling window is at least 200 px a side (4·√(50·50)), so on canvases under ~200 px it is the whole canvas and best_offset often has no room; then the membrane fill is what runs. The fallback test uses a 12 x 12 canvas for exactly that.
  - bestOffset needs the displaced domain bounds plus the ring fully inside the window, so a match needs at least ring pixels of margin on the far side.
  - PhotoCraft's History Brush source is a whole history state (paint_source); paint's undo stack only stores one layer's snapshot per entry, so US-078 uses per-layer snapshots instead of rebuilding states.

## 2026-10-24 - US-079
- Red Eye. paint/retouch.js: redEyeSearchRadius (4 + 1.2 × Pupil Size, clamped 8..128) and redEyeFix(data, W, H, cx, cy, pupilSize, darken, mask), a port of PhotoCraft redeye.rs apply over redeye_cmds.rs read_rect: the window is click ± (ceil(radius) + halo 5) clipped to the canvas; is_red_eye (R > G + 0.15, R > B + 0.15, Rec.709 luma < 0.92, chroma ≥ 0.08) inside the search disc, nearest seed, 4-connected flood, 1 px erode (window edge counts as off), separable Gaussian σ 0.8, then r → (g + b) / 2 and × (1 − 0.45 t) with t = soft mask × Darken. Returns the pixels whose RGB bytes changed; mask limits writes; alpha is kept.
- paint/tools.js: TOOLS.redEye (icon 'eye', after Healing Brush) with Pupil Size and Darken Amount. A click runs redEyeFix on a copy; a miss (0 changed) pushes no undo and draws 'No red-eye pixels near the click' with drawCloneHint; a hit runs pushUndo('Red Eye'), drops a float's baseData, then puts. paint/icons.js: eye. paint/paint.js: redEye on the keep-selection list. It is a click tool, so it is not in DRAG_STROKE_TOOLS.
- tests/paint-red-eye.test.mjs (8 tests). npm test: 2487 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Every story passed after this, so I appended US-080 (Pattern Stamp).
- Files changed: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/icons.js, paint/paint.js, tests/paint-red-eye.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft's red test is loose: a warm skin tone like (224, 172, 140) passes R > G + 0.15 and floods with the pupil. Test fixtures need a background with R − G ≤ 0.15 (PhotoCraft uses a brown iris).
  - The σ 0.8 blur feathers the correction about 3 px past the eroded pupil, so "background unchanged" assertions must skip that ring.
  - Darken 0 means t = 0 everywhere, so it changes nothing (and the tool shows the miss hint), same as PhotoCraft.
  - PhotoCraft's Pattern Stamp lives in crates/engine/src/retouch_cmds/pattern_stamp.rs; paint has no pattern presets, so US-080 uses a built-in tile plus Edit > Define Pattern.

## 2026-10-24 - US-083
- Free Transform (PhotoCraft edit.freeTransform). paint/transform.js: the bilinear premultiplied sampler is now a shared bilinear(...) helper (rotateImageData uses it, results unchanged). New pure functions: freeTransformAffine(box, { tx, ty, sx, sy, angle }) → [a, b, c, d, e, f]; transformImageData(img, box, affine, W, H) inverse-maps output pixel centres into the box and samples with edge pixels repeated inside the box, transparent outside it; transformedCorners; liftForTransform(img, mask, fill) → { source, base }; opaqueBBox; hitFreeTransform (eight handles, 'move' inside, 'rotate' outside); dragFreeTransform (the opposite handle stays fixed, Shift = uniform scale or 15° rotation snap).
- paint/paint.js: startFreeTransform (Edit > Free Transform, Ctrl/Cmd+T) lifts the selection (or the layer's opaque bbox). The hole is filled with the background color on the bottom layer and is transparent elsewhere; a floating paste's hole comes from sel.baseData. Live preview redraws the layer plus the box and handles on the overlay. Enter or a double-click commits: restore the original, pushUndo('Free Transform'), write the pixels. Escape and Ctrl+Z cancel without an undo row and restore the selection. A tool switch or any other pushUndo commits first. paint/pointer.js routes down / move / up to state.freeTransformPointer. paint/index.html: #btn-free-transform in the Edit menu. paint/history.js: 'Free Transform' → 'maximize-2'. paint/icons.js: maximize-2.

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - A commit drops the selection; PhotoCraft transforms the selection outline too. Keeping the outline would mean transforming the mask with the same affine (transformImageData on a one-channel mask). That could be a follow-up story.
  - Browsers usually keep Cmd/Ctrl+T for a new tab, so the Edit menu item is the dependable entry point. The keydown handler checks Ctrl+T before the plain 't' (Text tool) shortcut.
  - The uncommitted .tool-btn padding fix in paint/style.css and tests/paint-tool-icons.test.mjs is still in the working tree. I left it unstaged again.

## 2026-10-24 - US-087
- Exposure (PhotoCraft Adjustment::Exposure, 8-bit RGB path). paint/adjustments.js: applyExposure(imgData, params, mask) builds a 256-entry LUT: lin = max(0, (v / 255)^2.2 · 2^exposure + offset), lin^(1 / gamma) when gamma ≠ 1 (gamma floored at 0.01), encoded back through 1 / 2.2 and clamped. RGB only, alpha untouched, alpha-0 pixels skipped (map_rgb), mask honored, identity params return early. Registered as ADJUSTMENTS.exposure with Exposure (±20 stops, step 0.01), Offset (±0.5, step 0.0001), and Gamma Correction (0.1..9.99, default 1), PhotoCraft's slider ranges.
- paint/adjust-modal.js: 'exposure' in the Adjust menu order after Levels. paint/adjustment-layers.js: ADJUSTMENT_LAYER_IDS gains 'exposure' and 'vibrance' after 'levels' (Photoshop's order). paint/index.html: Layer > New Adjustment Layer lists Exposure… and Vibrance…. Defaults come from the fields, so both are identity.
- tests/paint-exposure.test.mjs (7 tests). tests/paint-adjustment-layers.test.mjs: the menu label list now has eight entries. npm test: 2560 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- Every story passed after this, so I appended US-088 (Color Balance adjustment and adjustment layer).
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, paint/adjustment-layers.js, paint/index.html, tests/paint-exposure.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-adjustment-layers.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft's Exposure linearizes RGB through a pure 2.2 power, not the sRGB curve (Transfer::for_exposure). The adjust.rs comment cites a ground truth: offset 0.1738 lifts 76 to 134. Use it as a test value.
  - A new adjustment layer kind has to be added in three places that a test checks against each other: ADJUSTMENT_LAYER_IDS, the data-layer="adjust-<id>" buttons in paint/index.html (same order), and the label list in tests/paint-adjustment-layers.test.mjs.
  - renderFields already handles fractional steps (input.step = f.step), so float params need no dialog change.

## 2026-10-24 - US-089
- Curves (PhotoCraft Adjustment::Curves, 8-bit RGB, smooth not pencil). paint/adjustments.js: curveLut(points) is PhotoCraft curve_lut (natural cubic spline in f64 through f32 knots, sorted and deduped, constant outside the end points, clamped, 4096-entry Float32Array); curvesByteLuts(params) builds each channel's row by running its curve through the master's table (sampleCurveLut is PhotoCraft lut(), f32 lerp), then samples it at v / 255 into a 256-entry LUT. applyCurves skips alpha-0 pixels, honors the mask, and returns early when all four curves are identity. Params are flat keys master / red / green / blue, each an array of { input, output } in 0..255 (PhotoCraft stores 0..1; the conversion is inside curveLut). Registered as ADJUSTMENTS.curves with four { type: 'curve' } fields defaulting to the frozen IDENTITY_CURVE.
- New paint/curve-editor.js (pure): graphToLevel / levelToGraph, isOffGraph, hitTestCurve, insertCurvePoint, moveCurvePoint (clamped strictly between neighbours), removeCurvePoint (never the end points), pickOrInsertCurvePoint, dragCurvePoint (always from the pointer-down points, so dragging a removed point back in restores it).
- paint/adjust-modal.js: renderFields collects the 'curve' fields into one editor (renderCurveEditor): a Channel select (RGB / Red / Green / Blue from the field labels) over a 256 × 256 canvas drawn by drawCurveGraph (quarter grid, diagonal, spline from curveLut, points). Styles are inline so style.css stays untouched. 'curves' is in the Adjust menu after Levels. paint/adjustment-layers.js: 'curves' after 'levels'. paint/index.html: Curves… in New Adjustment Layer.

## 2026-10-24 - US-091
- Photo Filter: applyPhotoFilter(imgData, params, mask) and photoFilterMatrix(color, density) in paint/adjustments.js, a port of PhotoCraft photo_filter_matrix and the 8-bit PhotoFilter arm. The pixel goes sRGB decode → XYZ_D50_TO_SRGB · diag(s) · SRGB_TO_XYZ_D50 → encode, then setLum to the original W3C luminosity when Preserve Luminosity is on, then a clamp. Registered as 'photoFilter' (color #ec8a00, density 25 over 0..100, preserveLuminosity true, PhotoCraft defaults). ## 2026-10-24 - US-102
- Brightness / Contrast now uses PhotoCraft's legacy math: applyLegacyBrightnessContrast(imgData, brightness, contrast, mask = null) (adjust.rs legacy_brightness_contrast_at, pivot 128/255, a 256-entry table). Brightness pre-adds before raised contrast and post-adds after lowered contrast. Alpha, transparent pixels, and masked-out pixels are untouched.
- ADJUSTMENTS.brightness: Brightness -150..150, Contrast -100..100, Use Legacy checkbox (default false). Unticking it clamps contrast up to -50 via the checkbox onChange (adjust_editors.rs brightness_contrast).
- Until the modern curve story (US-103) lands, legacy false also runs the legacy function. applyBrightness / applyContrast had no other callers and are removed.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, tests/paint-brightness-contrast.test.mjs (new), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt

  - Old saved Brightness params meant amount × 2.55 levels; they now mean levels directly (PhotoCraft's scale). Saved projects with nonzero brightness will look a little different.
  - The .tool-btn padding fix in paint/style.css and tests/paint-tool-icons.test.mjs is still uncommitted in the working tree. I left both unstaged.

## 2026-10-25 - US-103
- Brightness / Contrast with Use Legacy unticked now runs PhotoCraft's modern curves: modernBrightness(v, b) (adjust.rs Brightness: knee 126/255, slope 1.375^(|b|/50), end slope 1/(1 + 0.084|b|), Hermite or power roll-off, lowered = 32-step bisection inverse), modernContrast(v, c) (symmetric Hermite at 0.5, k = c/128), and applyModernBrightnessContrast(imgData, b, c, mask = null), a 256-entry table with contrast floored at -50. Alpha, transparent, and masked-out pixels are untouched.
- ADJUSTMENTS.brightness.apply: legacy === true → applyLegacyBrightnessContrast, anything else (including a missing key) → modern.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, tests/paint-brightness-contrast-modern.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-brightness-contrast.test.mjs (dropped the US-102 assertion that legacy false used legacy math), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- npm test: 2653 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- This was the last open story, so I appended US-104 (Hue / Saturation per-range Edit select, PhotoCraft HueRange default bounds).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Plain doubles meet PhotoCraft's Photoshop tolerances for these curves; Math.fround was not needed.
  - The lowered-brightness table uses per-entry bisection (256 × 32 evaluations), not PhotoCraft's 4× fine-sample monotone pass. The values agree within the test tolerances.
  - Saved projects whose Brightness / Contrast has legacy false or no legacy key now render with the modern curve.
  - The .tool-btn padding fix in paint/style.css and tests/paint-tool-icons.test.mjs is still uncommitted in the working tree. I left both unstaged.

## 2026-10-25 - US-104
- Hue / Saturation gains PhotoCraft's Edit select (dialog-local: Master, Reds, Yellows, Greens, Cyans, Blues, Magentas). Master shows hue / saturation / lightness; each range shows <range>Hue / <range>Saturation / <range>Lightness, all default 0. Colorize stays visible for every Edit choice.
- paint/adjustments.js: HUE_RANGES, hueRangeWeight(i, deg) (HueRange::neutral(i).weight), rangeLightness(rgb01, l) (adjust.rs range_lightness). applyHueSatLight gains a 7th ranges argument (array of { hue, saturation, lightness } in HUE_RANGES order): non-grey pixels add the weighted range shifts from their original hue to the master values, saturation clamps to -1..1, the master path runs, then rangeLightness with the summed range lightness. Colorize ignores ranges.
- Files changed: paint/adjustments.js, tests/paint-hsl-ranges.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-hsl-colorize.test.mjs (field-order and exact-params assertions loosened for the new keys), scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Paint's master Hue / Saturation path is still paint's own (s * (1 + sat), lightness added after), not PhotoCraft's lighten-first / divide-by-(1 - s) path. Range shifts were added onto paint's master path as the story asked. Porting the master path would be a separate story.
  - Paint's Levels raises t to g; PhotoCraft raises it to 1/g with a shadow toe above 1. US-105 fixes it.
  - The .tool-btn padding fix in paint/style.css and tests/paint-tool-icons.test.mjs is still uncommitted in the working tree. I left both unstaged.

## 2026-10-25 - US-105
- Levels now ports PhotoCraft levels_q (quantum 255) and levels_gamma: exported levelsGamma(u, g) (u^(1/g), with the 2^g-slope toe a·u − b·u^2.2 above gamma 1), whole-level rounding on the stretch, gamma, and output range, and new Output black / Output white fields (outBlack / outWhite). Gamma range is 0.01..9.99. Transparent pixels are skipped.
- applyLevels keeps mask in the 5th slot; outBlack / outWhite follow it (default 0 / 255), so old callers and saved params without an output range are unchanged.
- Files: paint/adjustments.js, tests/paint-levels-photocraft.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-levels.test.mjs (gamma direction flipped), tests/paint-adjustment-layers.test.mjs (default params now include the output range).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Paint's old Levels raised t to g, the opposite of PhotoCraft; gamma above 1 now brightens.
  - The stretch step adds 1e-3 before flooring (PhotoCraft) so 1.5-level ties round up; f64 math matches the Photoshop rows without Math.fround.
  - Per-channel Levels, Auto, and the histogram are still later stories.

## 2026-10-25 - US-111
- Gradient Map stores params.stops ([location rounded to 0.001, '#rrggbb'], sorted; default black → white, frozen). startColor / endColor fields are gone; applyGradientMap reads gradientMapStops(params), so a params object with only startColor / endColor still maps as two stops, and fewer than two stops falls back to black → white (read_stops).
- New paint/gradient-stops.js: hexToUnit / unitToHex (adjust_params::hex), gradientMapColor (moved from adjustments.js, re-exported there), normalizeStops, gradientMapStops, unitStops, sampleStops, moveMapStop (rank kept across coincident stops), addStopAt (64 cap), addStopInWidestGap (last widest gap on ties, like Rust max_by), removeStop (keeps 2), setStopColor. All return new arrays.
- paint/adjust-modal.js: field type 'gradient' renders renderGradientMapEditor: a 300 × 44 canvas.gradient-map-strip (bar inset 8, top 2, bottom 26; 49-sample bar; triangle + 10 × 10 marker per stop, selected bordered in --accent), pointer down on a marker (±6 px, below the bar, last stop first) selects and drags it with its grab offset, pointer down elsewhere adds a sampled stop. Below: Color, Location (%), Remove (disabled at 2), Add Stop (disabled at 64). Reverse / Dither checkboxes unchanged.
- cancelAdjust / commitAdjust on adjustment layers use replaceParams so a legacy layer that gained params.stops loses it again on Cancel and in the undo snapshot.
- Files changed: paint/gradient-stops.js (new), paint/adjustments.js, paint/adjust-modal.js, tests/paint-gradient-map-stops.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-gradient-map.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- npm test: 2709 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- This was the last open story, so I appended US-112 (Gradient Map Preset picker from PhotoCraft's builtin gradient library).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft adds a stop on click (release without drag); paint adds on pointer down away from a marker, which is simpler with Pointer Events and gives the same result for a click.
  - Float noise: (0.6 - 0.2) / 0.8 is 0.49999… in f64, so a sampled stop there rounds to #7f7f7f, not #808080. Test sampled colors at exactly representable locations.
  - jsdom has MouseEvent but no PointerEvent; dispatch new window.MouseEvent('pointerdown', { clientX, clientY }) and the canvas's zero bounding rect maps clientX to canvas pixels through the `r.width || W` fallback.
  - The .tool-btn padding fix in paint/style.css and tests/paint-tool-icons.test.mjs is still uncommitted in the working tree. I left both unstaged.

  - The options bar select (paint/ui.js) is flat: no optgroups. Group names live in the option value, so preset names that repeat across groups stay unique.
  - Interpolating stop colors in byte space (round(c0 + (c1 - c0) * k) on 0..255 bytes) keeps the old two-color gradient bit-exact; going through 0..1 floats and * 255 can flip .5 roundings.

The stop sampler now returns unrounded bytes and rounding happens once at write, so output with both off is unchanged. onUp passes the options through after pushUndo('Gradient').

## 2026-10-25 - US-129

## 2026-10-25 - US-140
- Background Eraser (PhotoCraft paint.backgroundEraser / bg_erase.rs): TOOLS.backgroundEraser after magicEraser, icon 'eraser-background'; options Size, Sampling (iconButtons pipette / circle-dot / square), Limits (Discontiguous / Contiguous / Find Edges), Tolerance 0..100 %, Protect Foreground Color.
- backgroundEraseDab (paint/retouch.js) follows bg_erase.rs: max per-channel RGB distance against the pre-stroke src, soft last-quarter falloff except Find Edges, protect skips pixels within tolerance of the fg, keep_connected for Contiguous / Find Edges. Find Edges needed no simplification: PhotoCraft's only difference is the hard (non-soft) match.
- PhotoCraft's per-dab a ← a·(1 − k) is the per-stroke cov accumulation c ← c + k(1 − c) with a = src_a·(1 − cov), so the dab rewrites from the pre-stroke src like eraseDab. Colour is kept (PhotoCraft zeroes it at a = 0; the canvas drops it anyway).
- Sampling: Continuous hotspotColor per dab, Once the first dab's hotspot (a transparent one makes the stroke a no-op), Background Swatch state.bgColor. Hardness is 1 (the options bar has none). Undo pushed on down; strokeSelectionMask clips; added to the "don't commit selection" list and DRAG_STROKE_TOOLS.
- Refactor: colorReplaceDab's contiguous flood moved into the shared keepConnected helper.
- Files: paint/retouch.js, paint/tools.js, paint/icons.js (eraser-background, circle-dot), paint/paint.js, paint/pointer.js, tests/paint-background-eraser.test.mjs.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - A contiguous limit seeds from the pixel under the dab centre; if that pixel is protected or transparent, the dab erases nothing. Tests for Protect Foreground should use Discontiguous.
  - The round tip's anti-aliased rim reaches radius + 0.5, so "outside the brush" assertions need a pixel at least a pixel beyond it.

## 2026-10-25 - US-177
- Edit › Transform › Skew / Distort / Perspective (edit.transform.skew / distort / perspective) open Free Transform in that mode (ctx.freeTransform(mode)); while a box is open they switch its mode.
- The box stays the affine ft.t until a quad gesture; then ft.quad (TL, TR, BR, BL) takes over and the source box warps onto it. quadGesture(grab, mode, { ctrl, alt, shift }) is PhotoCraft apply_mode_drag / mode_allows / mode_mods: Distort moves corners only (plus move from inside); Skew slides a corner along its main edge (⌥ moves the opposite corner the other way) and an edge along itself, never rotates from outside; Perspective moves a corner on the drag's main axis and mirrors the corner sharing that side, edges scale, outside rotates. In Free Transform ⌘/Ctrl-drag a corner distorts, ⌘⌥⇧-drag a corner adds perspective, ⌘-drag an edge skews (⇧ along the edge, ⌥ opposite edge too).
- dragQuad(q0, gesture, grab, start, point, mods) in paint/transform.js does every quad drag (move, rotate about the centre with ⇧ 15° snap, unit-frame scale through the quad's homography, distort / perspective / skew). Distort, Perspective, and the Skew corner stop short of folding (keepConvex, transform_tool.rs keep_convex).
- Commit warps through warpQuad(src, quad, W, H, box, nearest), the inverse homography with the shared bilinear sampler. quadHomography(box, quad) is the document homography; Transform Again stores it as a 9-entry lastTransform.affine, and transformLayerPixels / transformMask route 9-entry matrices through warpQuad (mapThrough).
- Presets on an open quad box: flips swap corners in the box frame, turns rotate corners about the centre (presetQuad).
- pointer.js now passes { ctrl, alt } as freeTransformPointer's 5th argument.
- Files changed: paint/transform.js, paint/paint.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/commands.js, tests/paint-transform-perspective.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-edit-transform.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/parity.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 54.3% done

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Warp (US next) can reuse the ft.quad session idea: give ft a mesh instead of a quad, and keep the affine-until-needed switch in freeTransformPointer.
  - A lastTransform.affine of length 9 is a homography; anything that replays it must go through mapThrough-style dispatch, not transformImageData.

## 2026-10-25 - US-178
- Edit › Transform › Warp (edit.transform.warp) opens Free Transform in mode 'warp' (or switches an open box to it). The box becomes ft.mesh, PhotoCraft's one-patch BezierMesh: 16 control points, row-major j · 4 + i, built by meshFromBox(box, m) from the box as it stands (the affine or quad homography carries over, enter_warp). Leaving Warp for another mode drops the mesh (leave_warp). Enter commits one 'Warp' undo row, Esc cancels; the turns / flips do nothing while warping, and Transform Again keeps the last matrix.
- The overlay draws the surface's outline and thirds, the eight anchor-to-handle lines, square anchors and round handles. A press within the handle reach of one of the twelve border points drags it (dragMeshPoint = warp.rs drag_point: an anchor carries its handles and the inner point beside it, a handle the points across its line); a press on the surface pulls it (meshParamAt + pullMesh: least-squares share over the four inner points plus the border handles near the border, corners fixed, so the grabbed point follows the pointer exactly).
- meshWarp(src, points, W, H, box, nearest) in paint/transform.js is warp.rs warp_mesh_surface: the surface is forward-mapped on a grid of ~4 px cells sized by the mapped row / column lengths (meshLengths = mapped_lengths, max 512 cells), each cell is two triangles, and every pixel centre inside a triangle samples the box at the barycentric source point with the shared bilinear sampler (later triangles draw over earlier ones). The identity mesh reproduces the box exactly.
- Files changed: paint/transform.js, paint/paint.js, paint/commands.js, paint/history.js, tests/paint-transform-warp.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-edit-transform.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/parity.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 54.4% done
- npm test: 3225 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The Split Warp items can split ft.mesh into a multi-patch mesh; meshWarp / evalMesh / dragMeshPoint currently assume one patch (4 x 4), so generalise them with us / vs knots then (warp.rs locate).
  - A small box gets only a few 4 px cells, so a piecewise-linear corner can sample a neighbour pixel; pixel tests of a bent mesh want a box of 40 px or more.

## 2026-10-25 - US-180
- Image › Image Size… (Cmd+Alt+I, image.imageSize) opens #image-size-modal: a "W px × H px (was …)" readout, Width / Height typed in Pixels or Percent (one Units select), Constrain Proportions (on by default; the other side follows round(edited · orig other / orig edited), at least 1 px), and Resample: Bicubic (default), Nearest Neighbor, Bilinear. The method is remembered for the session. OK with an unchanged size does nothing.
- resample(img, w, h, method) in paint/image-size.js: separable, premultiplied RGBA floats, edge pixels repeat. Nearest is floor((o + 0.5) / s) (exact colors); Bilinear and Bicubic sample at (o + 0.5) / s − 0.5 clamped to the edge; Bicubic is Keys a = −0.5 (Catmull-Rom), weights normalised, output clamped to alpha. Reducing an axis by more than 2×: Bilinear box-averages straight to the target (PhotoCraft resample.rs), Bicubic box-averages to 2× the target then filters.
- paint.js imageSize() pushes one 'Image Size' stack undo and runs resampleCanvas(w, h, sample), which now takes the sampler (default resampleImageData, so Crop's W x H commit is unchanged); masks go through the same sampler. Crop frame is re-ensured afterwards.
- Files changed: paint/image-size.js (new), paint/image-size-dialog.js (new), paint/paint.js, paint/commands.js, paint/stack-history.js, paint/history.js, paint/index.html, tests/paint-image-size.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-image-menu.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/parity.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt
- Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 54.8% done
- npm test: 3239 tests, 4 failing, all in the three ignored files.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PhotoCraft's real Bicubic is Keys a = −0.75 with a 1/128 phase table; the story asked for a = −0.5, so pixel parity with PhotoCraft's Bicubic is approximate. Smoother / Sharper / Lanczos / Preserve Details are not listed.
  - A new menu item breaks the order assertion in tests/paint-image-menu.test.mjs; update the expected list.

## 2026-10-25 - US-188
- Layer groups (PhotoCraft LayerContent::Group). state.layers stays one flat array, bottom first; a group is a record with kind 'group' (createGroupLayer: name, visible, opacity, blendMode 'pass-through' by default, collapsed) sitting directly above its children, and every layer carries parentId. paint/layer-groups.js (new) reads it as a tree (layerTree, normalizeTree, displayRows, blockIndices, isEffectivelyVisible) and holds the commands as plans: insertGroupAbove (layer.new.group, insert_above the active layer), groupLayersPlan (group_layers: top-level selected layers into a collapsed group at the topmost one's place), ungroupPlan (children take the group's place, top child active), moveLayerPlan / applyMovePlan (drag onto a group row → into it at the top; a child dropped on its own group → out above it; on a layer → that layer's sibling in its place; a group never into itself, nothing under the Background).
- Compositing: compositeTree(nodes, base, ops) walks the tree for both backends. Pass-through composites children straight into the backdrop and below 100 % mixes with the backdrop it started from (compose lib.rs); any other mode renders children into a transparent buffer and blends it on. flattenToCanvas runs it on canvases; compositeTreeBytes on RGBA bytes with blendRgb (tests). Any group switches the live view to the one composited canvas (syncCompositeView).
- Commands: Layer › New › Group… and Group from Layers… open the New Layer dialog in group mode (Mode lists Pass Through first); Layer › Group Layers (Cmd+G) and Ungroup Layers (Shift+Cmd+G) — the shortcuts are PhotoCraft engine bindings, not in the catalog. Footer group button enabled: new empty group, or Group Layers on Shift-click / several selected (panels.rs). Panel rows indent by depth, group rows have a chevron disclosure (no history step) and a folder icon; the header Mode shows Pass Through for a group.
- Group-aware edits: duplicate / delete take the whole block (Duplicate Group / Delete Group rows), Merge Down stays inside one group and never merges a group, Merge Layers / Merge Visible include selected or visible groups (layers left in a merged group move up, reparentOrphans), Delete Hidden Layers removes a hidden group with its contents, Flatten / Background from Layer reset parentId. Painting tools refuse a group (groupToolRefusal in pointer.js, status message); adjustments, filters, fill, transforms, masks and Layer via Copy / Cut refuse it too (refuseGroupTarget, pixelTarget).
- .paintproj saves the tree: groups are { kind: 'group', …, collapsed, children: [...] } with children bottom first; flat older files load unchanged. Undo stack records carry parentId and collapsed.
- Files changed: paint/layer-groups.js (new), paint/layers.js, paint/paint.js, paint/project.js, paint/stack-history.js, paint/layer-multi.js, paint/commands.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/new-layer-dialog.js, paint/history.js, paint/icons.js (chevron-down / chevron-right), paint/style.css, tests/paint-layer-groups.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-layers-panel.test.mjs, scripts/ralph/parity.json, scripts/ralph/prd.json, scripts/ralph/progress.txt

  - Plans that change parentIds must not mutate before the caller pushes undo; moveLayerPlan returns { order, layer, parentId } and applyMovePlan does the write.

## 2026-10-25 - US-189
- Clipping masks (PhotoCraft commands.rs set_clipped, compose lib.rs composite_stack_at): layer.clipped. compositeTree (paint/layer-groups.js) now gathers a base and the clipped siblings above it: the base renders alone (ops.isolate, or a group's children in a fresh buffer), each clipped layer blends atop it in its own mode and opacity (ops.drawAtop / atopBytes, blend.rs composite_atop; clipped adjustment layers adjust the base only), and the result goes onto the backdrop in the base's mode and opacity. A hidden base hides its clipped layers; a clipped layer with no base draws as a normal one; an adjustment-layer base drops its clipped layers. A clipped layer switches the live view to the composited canvas. Merge Down of a clipped layer into its base uses source-atop.

## 2026-10-25 - US-192
- Layer › Layer Style › Outer Glow… / Inner Glow… / Inner Shadow…: new kinds in EFFECT_KINDS / EFFECT_DEFAULTS (PhotoCraft effect_defaults: screen #ffffbe 75 % for glows, multiply black 75 % for the inner shadow) / EFFECT_FIELDS, so the commands, the dialog list, and .paintproj pick them up. Softer glows follow glow_map: spread / choke + tent blur (spreadSoften, shared with the drop shadow), then the Linear contour over Range (min(v / range, 1)); Source: Center inverts the edge glow. Inner Shadow follows shadow_map with the inverse shape shifted from the light and choked.
- Inner effects are built on a padded grid where everything past the canvas counts as outside (padShifted / unpad), so a layer that fills the canvas glows along its border as in PhotoCraft. They paint onto the body at per-pixel coverage, keeping alpha. Body order is Color Overlay, Inner Glow, Inner Shadow, Stroke (compose effects.rs).
- renderEffectParts now returns { under: [{ image, mode }], body } instead of { shadow, body }; layers.js (styledLayerParts, merge) and layer-groups.js byte compositor loop over `under`. The dialog's select fields come from one choices map (blend, position, source).
- Files: paint/layer-style.js, paint/layer-style-dialog.js, paint/layers.js, paint/layer-groups.js, tests/paint-layer-style-glow.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-layer-style.test.mjs (dialog list order).
- Parity: Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 65.2% done
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Satin, Gradient Overlay, Pattern Overlay, Bevel & Emboss are the remaining Layer Style kinds: add a kind to the four tables and either an `under` push (exterior) or an overlayColor(…, cov) pass in body order (interior).
  - Only the Softer glow technique exists; Precise and contours / noise / anti-alias are not modeled.

## 2026-10-25 - US-193
- Layer › Layer Style › Bevel & Emboss… / Satin… / Gradient Overlay… / Pattern Overlay… / Blending Options…. New kinds in EFFECT_KINDS / EFFECT_DEFAULTS (PhotoCraft effect_defaults) / EFFECT_FIELDS, so commands, dialog list (Photoshop order, Blending Options first, no checkbox), and .paintproj pick them up.
- Bevel & Emboss: bevelShading (paint/layer-style.js) follows bevel_geom / bevel_height / bevel_maps: Smooth = shape tent-blurred over the bevel width, Chisel Hard = bevel_chisel_h ramps from the EDT, Soften, lit by angle / altitude, Up / Down. Inner Bevel paints on the body; Outer Bevel adds highlight / shadow `under` images; Emboss / Pillow Emboss do both halves. Built on a padded grid so a canvas-filling layer bevels at the border.
- Satin = |tent(shape shifted +d) − tent(shape shifted −d)|, Invert, Linear contour. Gradient Overlay uses gradientT (PhotoCraft gradient_t: linear / radial / angle / reflected / diamond over the layer's alpha bounds) with stops + opacityStops; the dialog's Gradient select resolves a GRADIENT_PRESET_GROUPS preset with the app's fg / bg. Pattern Overlay snapshots Edit › Define Pattern's tile (or the checker stand-in) into fx.pattern as plain JSON; Scale and Link with Layer.
- Fill Opacity is styles.fillOpacity (percent, kept only below 100). renderEffectParts now builds the body in a relative float buffer (lay alpha = Fill inside the shape), paints interior effects with W3C compositing (paintLay), multiplies by the shape alpha, then lays the outside stroke under. Drop Shadow / Outer Glow are knocked out behind a see-through Fill. drawsStyle(layer) (effects on, or Fill < 100) replaces hasActiveEffects in the compositors and syncCompositeView. Blending Options' Opacity / Blend Mode are the layer's own: dialog open takes { general }, onPreview / onOk get (styles, general).
- Files: paint/layer-style.js, paint/layer-style-dialog.js, paint/layers.js, paint/layer-groups.js, paint/paint.js, paint/commands.js, paint/project.js, paint/gradient-presets.js (sampleOpacity exported), tests/paint-layer-style-rest.test.mjs (new), tests/paint-layer-style.test.mjs (dialog list).
- Parity: Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 66.1% done
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Interior effects go through paintLay(lay, inside, colorAt, k, mode, cov) on the relative buffer; never write body bytes before the shape alpha multiply, or a Fill below 100 % will fade the effect too.
  - .paintproj saves styles whenever cloneStyles(layer.styles) is non-null (a Fill-only record has no effect kinds, so hasLayerStyle is false for it).
  - Not modeled: contours other than Linear, Global Light (bevel uses its own angle), bevel texture / contour elements, gradient overlay Align off / offset, pattern angle, Blend Interior Effects as Group.

## 2026-10-25 - US-196
- Layer › Smart Objects › Convert to Smart Object / New Smart Object via Copy / Convert to Layers / Rasterize. A smart layer is kind 'smart' with layer.smart = { source, layers, transform } (paint/smart-objects.js): the composite and the content layers cropped to their opaque bounds, plus a 3x3 document homography. The canvas is always rendered from source through transform (placePixels: exact byte copy for a whole-pixel placement, bilinear for affine, warpQuad for perspective).
- Convert follows smart_cmds.rs convert / layer_to_smart: the selected top-level layers (masks applied, styles inside) become one smart object in the topmost one's place, named after it; a single layer keeps visibility, opacity, blend, clipping, locks on the smart layer. Via Copy is duplicateLayer with its own undo label (contents are immutable, so the copy is independent). Convert to Layers (unpack.rs) puts the contents back at the placement: one Normal layer folds in the smart layer's name / opacity / blend / mask, several go in a group named after it. Rasterize keeps the rendered canvas as a pixel layer.
- Free Transform on a smart layer edits the placement: the box is smartBounds (the placed contents' float bounds), the preview renders from the source, and Enter composes freeTransformHomography(box, t, quad) into smart.transform (tidy snaps near-integers, so 25 % then 400 % is the original placement and the original bytes). Edit › Transform presets / Again do the same; Warp is refused on a smart layer. Painting tools (smartToolRefusal in pointer.js) and pixel commands (refuseGroupTarget) refuse a smart layer unless its mask is the target.
- Undo: Convert to Smart Object / New Smart Object via Copy / Convert to Layers / Rasterize Smart Object are stack rows; single-layer snapshots (Free Transform) carry layer.smart; remountStack restores it. .paintproj saves { kind: 'smart', smart } with PNG data URLs for source and content layers; older files load unchanged.
- Files: paint/smart-objects.js (new), paint/layers.js, paint/paint.js, paint/commands.js, paint/pointer.js, paint/project.js, paint/stack-history.js, paint/style.css (smart thumbnail corner), tests/paint-smart-object.test.mjs (new).
- Parity: Paint vs PhotoCraft (browser scope): 68.2% done
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - transformSmart(smart, m) composes a document-space homography; affineHomography converts transform.js's canvas-order [a, b, c, d, e, f].
  - Not modeled: Edit Contents, smart filters, Stack Mode, linked contents, and re-placing a smart object after Crop / Canvas Size with an offset anchor (refreshSmartLayers re-renders at the new size in the old document coordinates). The Move tool is refused on a smart layer rather than moving its placement.
  - Not caused by this story: the uncommitted share-cluster `.menu-bar` / `.related-projects-*` edits in paint/style.css and tests/paint-layout-menu.test.mjs were left out of this commit.
